# Haftarah 2.0, Wave 2: the Torah reading and the library — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Put the week's Torah selection beside its haftarah, from the Hebcal calendar to two printed PDFs and one web page, make each year's sheet start from the last one ("Last time", carry-forward, "See also"), and turn the site's flat list into a library by parashah, by book and by special Shabbat with short addresses (`/noach`, `/5787/noach`).

**Architecture:** Congregation choices move into data (`customs/crc.json`, read by fetch and lint). `fetch.mjs` reads Hebcal's leyning item through a pure `scripts/leyning.mjs` (aliyot, maftir, the selection Daniel picks) and writes schema 3 with a `torah` reading first and the `haftarah` reading second, each with its own candidates and texts files. Every script reaches one reading through `readingView(data, id)`, so the haftarah of any week, with or without a Torah reading, builds the same `sheet.html` byte for byte; the Torah PDF is the same template with one build option (`reading: "torah"`) that `render.js` honours in three guarded places the haftarah build never reaches. A pure `scripts/prior.mjs` finds last year's sheet in the public index (read-only) and carries what fits. The library is client-side: a committed `site/app/parashot.js` (54 parashot, combined pairs, books, special tags) drives both the `vercel.json` alias allow-list and `site/app/library.js`, which resolves aliases and builds the landing page's four doors from `index.json`.

**Tech Stack:** Node 18+, zero npm dependencies, `node --test`, headless Chrome/Edge `--dump-dom` (through `scripts/chrome.mjs`), Hebcal leyning API (CC BY 4.0), Sefaria v3 texts and links APIs, Vercel static hosting with `vercel.json` rewrites, Vercel Blob (PUT and list API v13).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-web-2-0-design.md` (sections 2, 3, 4, 5.4, 6, 8, 9 are this wave; open item 11.4 is still open). Background: `docs/superpowers/research/2026-10-15-web-2-0/torah-and-library.md` (all of it) and `codebase-readiness.md` (sections 1.1, 1.2, 6). Format and earlier decisions: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-web-2-0-wave-1.md`.

## Global Constraints

- Node 18+, no npm dependencies, no `npm install`, no build tooling beyond `node scripts/*.mjs`; the site is plain HTML/CSS/ES modules served as files, no framework.
- **The printed sheet stays byte-identical for schema-2 data, and the haftarah of any week prints the same whether or not the week also has a Torah reading.** `template/sheet.css`, `template/sheet.html`, `template/trope.css` and the fonts are not edited. `template/render.js` is edited once, in Task 6, and only inside `opts.reading === "torah"` branches the haftarah build never passes; that task re-records the one `template/render.js` source hash with `node scripts/golden.mjs --update-sources` and shows with `git diff tests/golden/print.json` that nothing else moved.
- **The print golden** (`tests/golden/print.json`) has a `sources` layer: the sha256 of each template file and font, of every `lib.js` export (`sources.lib`), and, from Task 1, of every module-level helper and constant inside `lib.js` that is not exported (`sources.libInternal`). Wave 2 may ADD `lib.js` exports and helpers; it must not change an existing one unless the task explicitly re-records that hash with `node scripts/golden.mjs --update-sources` and verifies no fixture hash moved. Plain `--update` is never run in Wave 2. Existing fixture entries (`week-min`, `week-notes`) never move; the new `week-torah` entry is added with `--add week-torah` (Task 6).
- Week data (`weeks/`, JPS text) never enters the repo. Fixtures and test strings are synthetic or derived from the existing synthetic fixtures; never paste JPS English into a test, a fixture, a doc or a commit. Hebcal calendar data (book, chapter and verse numbers, reasons) may appear in tests.
- Tokens are never printed, logged, or put in error messages or test output.
- **Network:** only `fetch.mjs`, `publish.mjs` and `deliver.mjs` reach the network, and every function that does takes an injectable `fetchImpl` (or `getJSON`). No test reaches the network beyond `127.0.0.1`. The public index (`https://haftarah.centralreform.org/data/index.json`) is read-only from fetch: GET only, never written; only `publish.mjs` writes the index, through the Blob API.
- **Test isolation:** any test file that starts another program (child_process, or `buildFixture` from `scripts/golden.mjs`) imports `./helpers/isolatedEnv.mjs`, calls `isolatedEnv()`, passes its `env`, and never reads `process.env`.
- `npm test` passes (0 fail) at the end of every task, with Chrome present; without Chrome the browser tests report as skipped, never as failed.
- **Customs are data.** Every CRC choice (Diaspora calendar, haftarah rite, Torah selection rule, nusach, Hebrew and English versions, series name, credits, entry cap) lives in `customs/crc.json`. No CRC constant is added to `template/lib.js`, `scripts/fetch.mjs`, `scripts/leyning.mjs`, `scripts/lint.mjs`, `scripts/prior.mjs` or any `site/app/` module; scripts take `--customs <id>` and default to `crc`. The values Daniel has not confirmed (spec 11.4: the Torah custom, the entry cap, the nusach) are listed in the file's `toConfirm`, and fetch prints them on every run until he clears the list.
- No inline `<script>` and no `style` attribute anywhere in `site/` (the CSP forbids both); scripts position things through the CSSOM.
- Accessibility floor (Wave 1's): every interactive thing is a `<button>` or `<a>`, visible focus ring, Escape closes what is open, touch targets at least 44px (2.75rem), `lang="he" dir="rtl"` on Hebrew, `aria-pressed` on toggle buttons, color never the only signal (year dots carry screen-reader text; the current week has words, not just a ring).
- Design tokens are DESIGN.md's; nothing new except the year dot (rubric) and the current-cell ring (rubric, 1.5px).
- **Hebcal credit (CC BY 4.0).** Checked: README says only "Schedule: Hebcal leyning API.", and neither the colophon nor the web footer credits Hebcal. Task 13 adds "Calendar and readings: Hebcal (CC BY 4.0)." to README's "Sources and licenses" and to every web page footer. The print colophon gains it in Wave 4, with the one print change (spec 8: print additions wait for Wave 4).
- **User-visible strings** are exactly these (drafts for Daniel to see before the deploy, spec 11.5): reading headings "Torah" and "Haftarah"; the hero's "The printed sheets (PDF):" with links "Torah" and "Haftarah"; "Download the Torah sheet (PDF)"; "Last time: the <year> sheet"; the masthead "Torah and haftarah · <date>" before the text; the doors "By week", "By parashah", "By book", "Special Shabbatot"; "No sheet for <Parashah> yet.", "No sheet for <Parashah> in <year>.", "No sheets here yet."; list titles "Torah: <Book>", "Haftarot from <Book>", and the special's name ("Shabbat Shuvah"); the cell's screen-reader text "<n> sheet(s): <years>" and "; this week"; the footer line "Calendar and readings: Hebcal (CC BY 4.0)."; the data sentence "See also the <year> sheet: haftarah.centralreform.org/<year>/<key>." (spec 5.4); Drive names "<date> <Parashah> Torah — <ref>.pdf" and "<date> <Parashah> Haftarah — <ref>.pdf" for weeks with a Torah reading.
- Every commit message ends with the attribution trailer the executing session is given.

## Review Focus

Inputs the spec implies that a person will meet and no other task's tests would otherwise exercise; each line's test is added to the owning task.

1. **A double parashah week** (Vayakhel-Pekudei, Matot-Masei): the index row lists both halves in `parashahKeys`, `/vayakhel` and `/pekudei` open the combined week when it is the latest, both grid cells get its year dot, and next year's separate Vayakhel finds it as "Last time". → Task 7 test "indexEntry lists both halves of a combined parashah…", Task 8 test "findPrior: a combined week is last time for either half…", Task 10 test "resolveWeekAlias: either half of a combined parashah…".
2. **A special Shabbat whose Torah reading differs from the parashah's** (Chanukah or Rosh Chodesh: the maftir comes from Numbers): the aliyot list shows the special maftir with Hebcal's reason, a selection inside the parashah never silently includes it (the maftir is a pick of its own, `aliyah:M`; `aliyah:7-M` is refused), and the week is tagged for `/special/chanukah`. → Task 3 test "selectTorah on Shabbat Chanukah…", Task 7 test "weekTags…".
3. **A week with no prior** (the first year of the library, a new parashah, the index unreachable): fetch writes `prior.json` saying so and why, the digest's "Last time" says none, the sheet gets no `prior` and no "See also", the web hero shows no link, and nothing throws. → Task 8 test "no prior: …", Task 12 web-check line "no Last time link without a prior".
4. **A schema-2 week through every new code path:** lint, build, check, deliver (old Drive name), publish (no Torah PDF asked for, index row with the new fields filled), carry-forward from it as a prior, the digest, and the web page (no reading headings) all behave as before. → Task 7 test "a schema-2 week publishes as before…" (deliver's Wave 1 tests keep holding the old Drive names), Task 2's "a one-reading week keeps its old messages", Task 4's "mergeWeek: a kept schema-2 week without a Torah pick is left exactly as it was", the golden (Task 6), Task 8's schema-2 prior test, and web-check's "no reading heading on a one-reading week" (Task 12).
5. **An alias for a parashah with no published week** (`/haazinu` before any Ha'azinu sheet; `/5790/noach`): the library shell says "No sheet for Ha'azinu yet." (or "…in 5790.") with a link to all sheets, makes no error and leaves the address alone; an alias outside the allow-list is the site's 404. → Task 10 test "resolveWeekAlias: a parashah or a year with no sheet…" and web-check lines "an alias with no sheet says so" and "an unknown alias is a 404".

### Task 1: Foundations carried over from Wave 1 (Sonnet)

The Wave 1 ledger parked six items "for before Wave 2". This task delivers all six.

**Files:**
- Modify: `scripts/golden.mjs`, `tests/print-golden.test.mjs`, `scripts/digest.mjs`, `tests/digest.test.mjs`, `scripts/fetch.mjs`, `tests/browser.test.mjs`, `README.md`
- Create: `scripts/rites.mjs`, `tests/rites.test.mjs`
- Re-record (command only): `tests/golden/print.json` (adds `sources.libInternal`; nothing else may move)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `record`, `compare`, `compareSources`, `sourceHashes`, `readGolden`, `FIXTURES`, `DOM_KEYS`, `GOLDEN_FILE` in `scripts/golden.mjs`; `L.readingView`, `L.parashahKey` from `template/lib.js`; `migrate3` from `scripts/migrate.mjs`.
- Produces:
  - `scripts/golden.mjs`: `libInternals(text: string, exported: Set<string>) → { [name]: sha256 }`; `planUpdate(mode: "dom"|"sources"|"add", want: Golden, got: Golden, name?: string) → Golden` (throws when the step would hide a change); `compare(want, got)` now walks `Object.keys(want.fixtures)`; `sourceHashes()` returns `{ files, lib, libInternal }`; CLI `--update-dom`, `--update-sources`, `--add <fixture>`.
  - `scripts/rites.mjs`: `HAFTARAH_RITES = { ashkenazi: { [parashahKey]: ref } }`, `defaultHaftarahFor(parashahName: string, rite = "ashkenazi") → string|null` (throws on an unknown rite).
  - `scripts/digest.mjs`: `digestInput(sheet)` never throws; a schema-3 sheet with no readings gives `{ book: undefined, verses: [] }`, exactly as no sheet does (decision: the digest degrades to texts.json's own verse list, as it always has without a sheet).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** Append to `tests/print-golden.test.mjs`, and add `libInternals, planUpdate` to its import from `../scripts/golden.mjs`:

```js
test("libInternals hashes lib.js's module-level helpers that are not exported, ignoring comments and blank lines", () => {
  const src = [
    "(function () {",
    '  "use strict";',
    "  const TETRA = /x/g;",
    "  // a comment",
    "  const HE_WORD = /[a-z]+/g;",
    "",
    "  function exported(a) {",
    "    const inner = 1;",
    "    return a + inner;",
    "  }",
    "  const helper = s => s.trim();",
    "  return { TETRA, exported };",
    "});",
  ].join("\n");
  const ex = new Set(["TETRA", "exported"]);
  const a = libInternals(src, ex);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(a).sort(), ["HE_WORD", "helper"]);
  assert.deepEqual(libInternals(src.replace("  // a comment", "  // another comment\n\n  // and one more"), ex), a);
  const changed = libInternals(src.replace("/[a-z]+/g", "/[a-z]*/g"), ex);
  assert.notEqual(changed.HE_WORD, a.HE_WORD);
  assert.equal(changed.helper, a.helper);
});

test("compareSources: a module-level helper of lib.js must hold too; a new one is allowed", () => {
  const want = { files: {}, lib: {}, libInternal: { HE_WORD: "1" } };
  assert.deepEqual(compareSources(want, { files: {}, lib: {}, libInternal: { HE_WORD: "1", fresh: "2" } }), []);
  assert.deepEqual(compareSources(want, { files: {}, lib: {}, libInternal: { HE_WORD: "2" } }), ["lib.js internal HE_WORD changed"]);
  assert.deepEqual(compareSources(want, { files: {}, lib: {}, libInternal: {} }), ["lib.js internal HE_WORD missing"]);
  assert.deepEqual(compareSources({ files: {}, lib: {} }, { files: {}, lib: {}, libInternal: { HE_WORD: "9" } }), [], "a golden recorded before the layer existed checks nothing there");
});

test("the recorded golden guards lib.js's internals (TETRA's neighbours: HE_WORD, BLOCK_ENDING, tropeTokens ...)", () => {
  assert.ok(golden.sources.libInternal, "tests/golden/print.json has no sources.libInternal; record it with node scripts/golden.mjs --update-sources");
  for (const n of ["HE_WORD", "consonantKey", "ENTITIES", "READING_OWN", "BLOCK_ENDING", "tropeTokens"]) assert.ok(n in golden.sources.libInternal, n);
});

const F = { html: "h", trope: "t", dom: "d", tropeDom: "td" };
const SRC = { files: { "template/sheet.css": "1" }, lib: { divineName: "1" }, libInternal: { HE_WORD: "1" } };
const WANT = { browser: "HeadlessChrome/155.0.0.0", platform: "win32", sources: SRC, fixtures: { "week-min": F } };

test("planUpdate dom: takes only what a browser update moved, and refuses while HTML or sources moved", () => {
  const got = { browser: "HeadlessChrome/156.0.0.0", platform: "win32", sources: SRC, fixtures: { "week-min": { ...F, dom: "d2", tropeDom: "td2" } } };
  assert.deepEqual(planUpdate("dom", WANT, got), { ...WANT, browser: "HeadlessChrome/156.0.0.0", fixtures: { "week-min": { ...F, dom: "d2", tropeDom: "td2" } } });
  assert.throws(() => planUpdate("dom", WANT, { ...got, fixtures: { "week-min": { ...F, html: "h2" } } }), /--update-dom refused: week-min html changed/);
  assert.throws(() => planUpdate("dom", WANT, { ...got, sources: { ...SRC, files: { "template/sheet.css": "2" } } }), /--update-dom refused: template\/sheet\.css changed/);
  assert.throws(() => planUpdate("dom", WANT, { ...got, browser: null }), /needs Chrome or Edge/);
});

test("planUpdate sources: takes the new sources only while every recorded fixture holds", () => {
  const got = { ...WANT, sources: { ...SRC, files: { "template/sheet.css": "2" } } };
  assert.deepEqual(planUpdate("sources", WANT, got), { ...WANT, sources: got.sources });
  assert.throws(() => planUpdate("sources", WANT, { ...got, fixtures: { "week-min": { ...F, dom: "moved" } } }), /--update-sources refused: week-min dom moved/);
  // on another browser the DOM is not compared, so only the HTML can refuse
  assert.deepEqual(planUpdate("sources", WANT, { ...got, browser: "HeadlessChrome/156.0.0.0", fixtures: { "week-min": { ...F, dom: "other" } } }).fixtures, WANT.fixtures);
});

test("planUpdate add: records one new fixture, never a recorded one, and only on the golden's browser", () => {
  const got = { ...WANT, fixtures: { "week-min": F, "week-notes": { html: "nh", trope: "nt", dom: "nd", tropeDom: "ntd" } } };
  assert.deepEqual(planUpdate("add", WANT, got, "week-notes").fixtures, got.fixtures);
  assert.throws(() => planUpdate("add", WANT, got, "week-min"), /already recorded/);
  assert.throws(() => planUpdate("add", WANT, got, "week-nothing"), /--add takes one of/);
  assert.throws(() => planUpdate("add", WANT, { ...got, fixtures: { ...got.fixtures, "week-min": { ...F, html: "x" } } }, "week-notes"), /--add refused: week-min html/);
  assert.throws(() => planUpdate("add", WANT, { ...got, browser: "HeadlessChrome/156.0.0.0" }, "week-notes"), /run --update-dom first/);
  assert.throws(() => planUpdate("add", WANT, { ...got, browser: null }, "week-notes"), /needs the browser the golden was rendered on/);
});
```

Create `tests/rites.test.mjs`:

```js
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { defaultHaftarahFor, HAFTARAH_RITES } from "../scripts/rites.mjs";

// Hebcal's English names for the 54 parashot and the 7 combined weeks.
const HEBCAL = ["Bereshit", "Noach", "Lech-Lecha", "Vayera", "Chayei Sara", "Toldot", "Vayetzei", "Vayishlach", "Vayeshev", "Miketz", "Vayigash", "Vayechi",
  "Shemot", "Vaera", "Bo", "Beshalach", "Yitro", "Mishpatim", "Terumah", "Tetzaveh", "Ki Tisa", "Vayakhel", "Pekudei",
  "Vayikra", "Tzav", "Shmini", "Tazria", "Metzora", "Achrei Mot", "Kedoshim", "Emor", "Behar", "Bechukotai",
  "Bamidbar", "Nasso", "Beha'alotcha", "Sh'lach", "Korach", "Chukat", "Balak", "Pinchas", "Matot", "Masei",
  "Devarim", "Vaetchanan", "Eikev", "Re'eh", "Shoftim", "Ki Teitzei", "Ki Tavo", "Nitzavim", "Vayeilech", "Ha'azinu", "Vezot Haberakhah",
  "Vayakhel-Pekudei", "Tazria-Metzora", "Achrei Mot-Kedoshim", "Behar-Bechukotai", "Chukat-Balak", "Matot-Masei", "Nitzavim-Vayeilech"];

test("every Hebcal parashah name finds its Ashkenazi haftarah, apostrophes and hyphens included", () => {
  for (const name of HEBCAL) assert.ok(defaultHaftarahFor(name), name);
  assert.equal(defaultHaftarahFor("Ha'azinu"), "II Samuel 22:1-51");      // missed before Wave 2: "ha azinu" was looked up
  assert.equal(defaultHaftarahFor("Sh'lach"), "Joshua 2:1-24");
  assert.equal(defaultHaftarahFor("Vayakhel-Pekudei"), "I Kings 7:51-8:21");
  assert.equal(defaultHaftarahFor("Pesach Shabbat Chol ha-Moed"), null);
  assert.equal(Object.keys(HAFTARAH_RITES.ashkenazi).length, 61);
  assert.throws(() => defaultHaftarahFor("Noach", "yemenite"), /no haftarah table for rite "yemenite"/);
});

test("fetch.mjs keeps no parashahKey of its own (lib.js's is the one)", () => {
  const src = readFileSync(new URL("../scripts/fetch.mjs", import.meta.url), "utf8");
  assert.ok(!/(const|let|function)\s+parashahKey\b/.test(src));
  assert.ok(!/DEFAULT_HAFTAROT|normalizeParashah/.test(src));
});
```

```js
test("digestInput never throws: a schema-3 sheet with no readings gives what no sheet gives", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(digestInput({ schema: 3, readings: [] }), { book: undefined, verses: [] });
  assert.deepEqual(digestInput({ schema: 3 }), { book: undefined, verses: [] });
  const md = buildDigest({ texts, candidates, ...digestInput({ schema: 3, readings: [] }) });
  assert.equal(md, buildDigest({ texts, candidates, ...digestInput(null) }));
});
```

```js
test("web-check passes on a schema-3 copy of week-notes (the browser path, not just Node)", { skip, timeout: 300_000 }, async () => {
  const iso = await isolatedEnv();
```

Add after `export const foldEol = …`:

```js
/**
 * The module-level helpers and constants of lib.js that are not exported, each hashed: the factory's
 * top-level `const`/`let`/`function` statements (two-space indent), each running to the next one or to
 * the closing `return {`. Comment lines and blank lines are dropped first, so a comment above a new
 * function moves nothing. Exported names are left to the "lib" layer.
 */
export function libInternals(text, exported) {
  const src = foldEol(text);
  const end = src.lastIndexOf("\n  return {");
  const starts = [...src.matchAll(/^ {2}(?:const|let|var|function)\s+([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)/gm)].filter(m => end < 0 || m.index < end);
  const out = {};
  starts.forEach((m, i) => {
    if (exported.has(m[1])) return;
    const stop = i + 1 < starts.length ? starts[i + 1].index : (end < 0 ? src.length : end);
    const body = src.slice(m.index, stop).split("\n").filter(l => !/^\s*(\/\/.*)?$/.test(l)).join("\n");
    out[m[1]] = sha256(body);
  });
  return out;
}
```

Add after `compare`:

```js
/**
 * What --update-dom, --update-sources and --add <fixture> write, from the recorded golden `want` and a
 * fresh recording `got`. Each refuses (throws) when it would hide a change it is not for:
 *   dom      only the DOM layer, after a browser update; the sources and every fixture's HTML must hold
 *   sources  only the sources layer (a deliberate template or lib.js change); every recorded fixture must hold
 *   add      one fixture not yet recorded; everything recorded must hold, on the golden's own browser
 */
export function planUpdate(mode, want, got, name = null) {
  if (mode === "dom") {
    if (!got.browser) throw new Error("--update-dom needs Chrome or Edge on this machine");
    const held = compare({ ...want, browser: null }, got);
    if (held.length) throw new Error(`--update-dom refused: ${held.join(", ")} changed; this step only re-records what a browser update moved`);
    const fixtures = {};
    for (const [n, f] of Object.entries(want.fixtures)) {
      fixtures[n] = { ...f };
      for (const k of DOM_KEYS) if (got.fixtures[n] && got.fixtures[n][k] !== undefined) fixtures[n][k] = got.fixtures[n][k];
    }
    return { ...want, browser: got.browser, platform: got.platform, fixtures };
  }
  if (mode === "sources") {
    const held = compare({ ...want, sources: undefined }, got);
    if (held.length) throw new Error(`--update-sources refused: ${held.join(", ")} moved; a source change may not move a recorded fixture`);
    return { ...want, sources: got.sources };
  }
  if (mode === "add") {
    if (!FIXTURES.includes(name)) throw new Error(`--add takes one of ${FIXTURES.join(", ")}`);
    if (want.fixtures[name]) throw new Error(`--add: ${name} is already recorded; recorded fixtures are compared, never re-recorded`);
    if (want.browser && !got.browser) throw new Error(`--add needs the browser the golden was rendered on (${want.browser})`);
    if (want.browser && (want.browser !== got.browser || want.platform !== got.platform)) throw new Error(`--add: the golden was rendered on ${want.browser} (${want.platform}) and this is ${got.browser} (${got.platform}); run --update-dom first`);
    const held = compare(want, got);
    if (held.length) throw new Error(`--add refused: ${held.join(", ")} moved`);
    const f = { ...got.fixtures[name] };
    if (!want.browser) for (const k of DOM_KEYS) delete f[k];
    return { ...want, fixtures: { ...want.fixtures, [name]: f } };
  }
  throw new Error(`unknown golden update "${mode}"`);
}
```

Replace `main()` with:

```js
async function main() {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  const mode = args.includes("--update") ? "all" : args.includes("--update-dom") ? "dom" : args.includes("--update-sources") ? "sources" : args.includes("--add") ? "add" : null;
  const got = await record();
  if (mode === "all") {
    await mkdir(dirname(GOLDEN_FILE), { recursive: true });
    await writeFile(GOLDEN_FILE, JSON.stringify(got, null, 2) + "\n", "utf8");
    console.log(`Recorded ${FIXTURES.length} fixtures to ${rel(GOLDEN_FILE)}${got.browser ? ` (rendered with ${got.browser} on ${got.platform})` : " (HTML only: no Chrome or Edge found)"}`);
    return;
  }
  const want = await readGolden();
  if (mode) {
    const name = mode === "add" ? args[args.indexOf("--add") + 1] : null;
    const out = planUpdate(mode, want, got, name);
    await writeFile(GOLDEN_FILE, JSON.stringify(out, null, 2) + "\n", "utf8");
    console.log(`Recorded ${mode === "add" ? `the ${name} fixture` : mode === "dom" ? `the DOM layer (${got.browser} on ${got.platform})` : "the sources layer"} in ${rel(GOLDEN_FILE)}`);
    return;
  }
  const diffs = compare(want, got);
  if (diffs.length) { console.error(`Print golden differs: ${diffs.join(", ")}`); process.exit(1); }
  const same = want.browser === got.browser && want.platform === got.platform;
  console.log(`Print golden holds${same ? "" : ` (HTML only: recorded on ${want.browser}/${want.platform}, this is ${got.browser}/${got.platform})`}.`);
}
```

```js
 * rites.mjs — each rite's default haftarah for every parashah, keyed by lib.js's parashahKey
 * ("lech-lecha", "haazinu", "vayakhel-pekudei"). fetch.mjs uses it only to say what the reading would

 */
import { createRequire } from "node:module";

export const HAFTARAH_RITES = {
  ashkenazi: {
    "bereshit": "Isaiah 42:5-43:10", "noach": "Isaiah 54:1-55:5", "lech-lecha": "Isaiah 40:27-41:16", "vayera": "II Kings 4:1-37",

    "vayeshev": "Amos 2:6-3:8", "miketz": "I Kings 3:15-4:1", "vayigash": "Ezekiel 37:15-28", "vayechi": "I Kings 2:1-12",
    "shemot": "Isaiah 27:6-28:13; 29:22-23", "vaera": "Ezekiel 28:25-29:21", "bo": "Jeremiah 46:13-28", "beshalach": "Judges 4:4-5:31",
    "yitro": "Isaiah 6:1-7:6; 9:5-6", "mishpatim": "Jeremiah 34:8-22; 33:25-26", "terumah": "I Kings 5:26-6:13", "tetzaveh": "Ezekiel 43:10-27",
    "ki-tisa": "I Kings 18:1-39", "vayakhel": "I Kings 7:40-50", "pekudei": "I Kings 7:51-8:21", "vayakhel-pekudei": "I Kings 7:51-8:21",
    "vayikra": "Isaiah 43:21-44:23", "tzav": "Jeremiah 7:21-8:3; 9:22-23", "shmini": "II Samuel 6:1-7:17", "tazria": "II Kings 4:42-5:19",
    "metzora": "II Kings 7:3-20", "tazria-metzora": "II Kings 7:3-20", "achrei-mot": "Ezekiel 22:1-19", "kedoshim": "Amos 9:7-15",
    "achrei-mot-kedoshim": "Amos 9:7-15", "emor": "Ezekiel 44:15-31", "behar": "Jeremiah 32:6-27", "bechukotai": "Jeremiah 16:19-17:14",
    "behar-bechukotai": "Jeremiah 16:19-17:14", "bamidbar": "Hosea 2:1-22", "nasso": "Judges 13:2-25", "behaalotcha": "Zechariah 2:14-4:7",
    "shlach": "Joshua 2:1-24", "korach": "I Samuel 11:14-12:22", "chukat": "Judges 11:1-33", "balak": "Micah 5:6-6:8",
    "chukat-balak": "Micah 5:6-6:8", "pinchas": "I Kings 18:46-19:21", "matot": "Jeremiah 1:1-2:3", "masei": "Jeremiah 2:4-28; 3:4",
    "matot-masei": "Jeremiah 2:4-28; 3:4", "devarim": "Isaiah 1:1-27", "vaetchanan": "Isaiah 40:1-26", "eikev": "Isaiah 49:14-51:3",
    "reeh": "Isaiah 54:11-55:5", "shoftim": "Isaiah 51:12-52:12", "ki-teitzei": "Isaiah 54:1-10", "ki-tavo": "Isaiah 60:1-22",
    "nitzavim": "Isaiah 61:10-63:9", "vayeilech": "Isaiah 55:6-56:8", "nitzavim-vayeilech": "Isaiah 61:10-63:9",
    "haazinu": "II Samuel 22:1-51", "vezot-haberakhah": "Joshua 1:1-18",
  },
};

/** The rite's haftarah for a parashah named as Hebcal names it, or null (a festival, an unknown name). */
export function defaultHaftarahFor(parashah, rite = "ashkenazi") {
  const table = HAFTARAH_RITES[rite];
  if (!table) throw new Error(`no haftarah table for rite "${rite}"`);
  return table[L.parashahKey(parashah)] || null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: digest.mjs.** Replace `digestInput` with:

```js
// What the digest needs from sheet.json: the book and the verse refs, read through readingView so a
// schema-3 week gives the same answer as its schema-2 original. It never throws: a sheet with no
// readings says nothing, as no sheet does, and the digest falls back to texts.json's own verses.
export function digestInput(sheet) {
  let view = null;
  try { view = sheet ? L.readingView(sheet) : null; } catch { view = null; }
  return { book: view?.haftarah?.book, verses: (view?.verses || []).map(v => v.ref).filter(Boolean) };
}
```

Create `tests/helpers/makeWeekTorah.mjs`:

```js
// The two-reading fixture, derived from week-min so no new text enters the repo: the haftarah is
// week-min migrated to schema 3, untouched, and the Torah reading is the same synthetic verses (and
// their gloss and classical entry) relabelled Genesis 6:9-11. The slug stays week-min's, so the
// haftarah's view is exactly migrate3(week-min)'s and must build the same sheet.html.
// tests/fixtures/week-torah/sheet.json is this function's output; tests/build.test.mjs holds them equal.
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import { migrate3 } from "../../scripts/migrate.mjs";
const L = createRequire(import.meta.url)("../../template/lib.js");

const ALIYOT = [
  { n: 1, ref: "Genesis 6:9-22", verses: 14 }, { n: 2, ref: "Genesis 7:1-16", verses: 16 }, { n: 3, ref: "Genesis 7:17-8:14", verses: 22 },
  { n: 4, ref: "Genesis 8:15-9:7", verses: 15 }, { n: 5, ref: "Genesis 9:8-17", verses: 10 }, { n: 6, ref: "Genesis 9:18-10:32", verses: 44 },
  { n: 7, ref: "Genesis 11:1-32", verses: 32 }, { n: "M", ref: "Genesis 11:29-32", verses: 4 },
];

export function makeWeekTorah(weekMin) {
  const week = migrate3(weekMin);
  const h = week.readings[0];
  const first = h.verses[0].verse;
  const to = v => 9 + (v - first);   // week-min is one chapter: 20:18 becomes 6:9
  const torah = {
    id: "torah", kind: "torah", ref: `Genesis 6:9-${to(h.verses[h.verses.length - 1].verse)}`, heRef: null, book: "Genesis",
    selection: "aliyah 1, its first verses", aliyot: ALIYOT, maftir: { ref: "Genesis 11:29-32" },
    incipit: { ...h.incipit }, title: { en: "Noach", he: (h.title && h.title.he) || "" }, versions: { ...h.versions },
    verses: h.verses.map(v => ({ ...v, book: "Genesis", chapter: 6, verse: to(v.verse), ref: `Genesis 6:${to(v.verse)}` })),
    glosses: h.glosses.map(g => ({ ...g, chapter: 6, verse: to(g.verse) })),
    commentary: h.commentary.filter(e => e.register !== "critical").map(({ id, ...e }) => ({
      ...e, chapter: 6, verse: to(e.verse), ...(e.verseEnd ? { verseEnd: to(e.verseEnd) } : {}),
      sourceRef: String(e.sourceRef || "").replace(/ on .*$/, ` on Genesis 6:${to(e.verse)}`),
    })),
  };
  L.assignEntryIds(torah.commentary, "torah", new Set(h.commentary.map(e => e.id)));
  week.readings.unshift(torah);
  return week;
}
```

```js
  assert.deepEqual(lintSheet(min).warnings, lint(min).warnings, "a one-reading week keeps its old messages");
});

  const cap = r.warnings.filter(w => /over the cap/.test(w.msg));
  assert.deepEqual(cap, [{ where: "haftarah", msg: "17 entries, over the cap of 10 (traditional 9, modern 4, critical 4, reference 0)" }]);
  assert.equal(r.errors.length, lint(d).errors.length);
  assert.deepEqual(r.counts, [{ reading: "haftarah", total: 17, cap: 10, traditional: 9, modern: 4, critical: 4, reference: 0 }]);
  assert.ok(!lintSheet(d, { customs: { entryCap: 24 } }).warnings.some(w => /over the cap/.test(w.msg)));
  const m = structuredClone(d); m.commentary[0].status = "rejected";
  assert.equal(entryCounts({ commentary: m.commentary }).total, 16, "a rejected entry is not counted");
  assert.equal(entryCounts({ commentary: [{ register: "something else" }] }).traditional, 1);
});
```

Create `scripts/customs.mjs`:

```js
/**
 * customs.mjs — a congregation's customs as data (spec 3.2): calendar, haftarah rite, how the Torah
 * selection is made, versions, series, credits, the entry cap. customs/<id>.json; CRC's is crc.json.
 * fetch.mjs and lint.mjs take the object; nothing CRC-specific lives in their code.
 *
 * `toConfirm` lists fields Daniel has not confirmed yet (spec 11.4); fetch prints them every run.
 */
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { join, resolve, dirname } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { HAFTARAH_RITES } from "./rites.mjs";

const ROOT = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");
export const CUSTOMS_DIR = join(ROOT, "customs");
export const TORAH_MODES = ["selection", "triennial", "full"];

/** Every problem that makes a customs record unusable, in field order; [] when it is fine. */
export function validateCustoms(c) {
  if (!c || typeof c !== "object" || Array.isArray(c)) return ["not a JSON object"];
  const str = v => typeof v === "string" && v.trim().length > 0;
  const p = [];
  if (!str(c.id) || !/^[a-z0-9-]+$/.test(c.id)) p.push("id: lowercase letters, digits and hyphens");
  if (typeof (c.calendar && c.calendar.diaspora) !== "boolean") p.push("calendar.diaspora: true or false");
  if (!Object.keys(HAFTARAH_RITES).includes(c.haftarahRite)) p.push(`haftarahRite: one of ${Object.keys(HAFTARAH_RITES).join(", ")}`);
  if (!TORAH_MODES.includes(c.torah && c.torah.mode)) p.push(`torah.mode: one of ${TORAH_MODES.join(", ")}`);
  if (!str(c.versions && c.versions.he) || !str(c.versions && c.versions.en)) p.push("versions.he and versions.en: Sefaria version titles");
  if (!Number.isInteger(c.entryCap) || c.entryCap < 1) p.push("entryCap: a whole number of entries per reading");
  if (!str(c.series && c.series.name)) p.push("series.name: the series the sheets belong to");
  if (c.credits != null && (typeof c.credits !== "object" || Array.isArray(c.credits))) p.push("credits: an object");
  if (c.toConfirm != null && !Array.isArray(c.toConfirm)) p.push("toConfirm: a list of field names");
  return p;
}

/** customs/<id>.json, or a path to a JSON file; throws naming the file and every problem. */
export async function loadCustoms(idOrPath = "crc") {
  const file = /[\\/]|\.json$/i.test(idOrPath) ? resolve(idOrPath) : join(CUSTOMS_DIR, `${idOrPath}.json`);
  let c;
  try { c = JSON.parse(await readFile(file, "utf8")); }
  catch (err) { throw new Error(`customs: cannot read ${file}: ${err.message}`); }
  const problems = validateCustoms(c);
  if (problems.length) throw new Error(`customs: ${file} is not usable:\n  - ${problems.join("\n  - ")}`);
  return c;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: lint.mjs.** Add `import { loadCustoms } from "./customs.mjs";` after the `node:module` import. Replace `lintSheet` (and its doc comment) with:

```js
/** A reading's printed entries by register (rejected ones left out; an unknown register is traditional, as it prints). */
export function entryCounts(reading) {
  const out = { total: 0, traditional: 0, modern: 0, critical: 0, reference: 0 };
  for (const e of (reading.commentary || []).filter(visible)) {
    out.total++;
    out[L.SIGLA[e.register] ? e.register : "traditional"]++;
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * Lint a sheet of either schema: every reading that has verses (or the ids in `only`), each through
 * readingView. When the week has more than one reading, every message names its reading. Above
 * customs.entryCap a reading gets a warning with its count by register; the cap never stops a build.
 * With fix, the gloss fixes are written into `file` itself (each view shares its reading's glosses).
 */
export function lintSheet(file, { voices = {}, fix = false, customs = null, only = null } = {}) {
  const all = L.readings(file).filter(r => (r.verses || []).length);
  const multi = all.length > 1;
  const out = { errors: [], warnings: [], fixes: [], counts: [] };
  for (const r of all) {
    if (only && !only.includes(r.id)) continue;
    const view = L.readingView(file, r.id);
    const res = lint(view, { voices });
    const tag = x => (multi ? { ...x, where: `${r.id}: ${x.where}` } : x);
    out.errors.push(...res.errors.map(tag));
    out.warnings.push(...res.warnings.map(tag));
    if (fix && res.fixes.length) applyFixes(view, res.fixes);
    out.fixes.push(...res.fixes.map(f => (multi ? { ...f, reading: r.id } : f)));
    const c = entryCounts(r);
    const cap = customs && customs.entryCap ? customs.entryCap : null;
    out.counts.push({ reading: r.id, total: c.total, cap, traditional: c.traditional, modern: c.modern, critical: c.critical, reference: c.reference });
    if (cap && c.total > cap) out.warnings.push({ where: r.id, msg: `${c.total} entries, over the cap of ${cap} (${L.REGISTER_ORDER.map(k => `${k} ${c[k]}`).join(", ")})` });
  }
  return out;
}
```

```js
  const r = lintSheet(data, { voices: await loadVoices(), fix });

```

### Task 3: The Torah calendar and the selection (Opus)

A pure module over Hebcal's leyning item: the full aliyot list with the maftir (special Shabbat and festival overrides come with Hebcal's item), the triennial reading, and the selection Daniel picks. No network here; fetch makes the one call (Task 4).

**Files:**
- Create: `scripts/leyning.mjs`, `tests/leyning.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing from earlier tasks.
- Produces (`scripts/leyning.mjs`):
  - `hebcalRef({ k, b, e }) → "Genesis 6:9-22" | "Genesis 6:9-7:16" | "Exodus 30:11"`
  - `parseRange(ref) → { book, from: [ch, v], to: [ch, v] } | null` (one range in one book)
  - `aliyotOf(set) → [{ n: 1..7 | "M", ref, verses?, reason? }]`
  - `leyningOf(item) → Leyning = { parashah, type, summary, aliyot, triennial: { year, aliyot } | null, source: "Hebcal" }`
  - `parsePick(text) → { kind: "aliyah", from, to } | { kind: "triennial" } | { kind: "full" }` (from/to are 1..7, or both "M"); throws on anything else
  - `defaultPick(customs) → pick | null` (`torah.mode` "triennial" or "full" pick for themselves; "selection" returns null: Daniel picks weekly)
  - `selectTorah(leyning, pick | { kind: "ref", ref }) → { ref, book, selection, maftir: { ref, reason? } | null }`
  - `aliyotLines(leyning) → string[]` (console), `aliyotMarkdown(leyning, torahReading | null) → string` (digest)

```js
const NOACH = { name: { en: "Noach", he: "נֹחַ" }, type: "shabbat", summary: "Genesis 6:9-11:32",
  fullkriyah: { 1: { k: "Genesis", b: "6:9", e: "6:22", v: 14 }, 2: { k: "Genesis", b: "7:1", e: "7:16", v: 16 }, 3: { k: "Genesis", b: "7:17", e: "8:14", v: 22 },
    4: { k: "Genesis", b: "8:15", e: "9:7", v: 15 }, 5: { k: "Genesis", b: "9:8", e: "9:17", v: 10 }, 6: { k: "Genesis", b: "9:18", e: "10:32", v: 44 },
    7: { k: "Genesis", b: "11:1", e: "11:32", v: 32 }, M: { k: "Genesis", b: "11:29", e: "11:32", v: 4 } },
  triennial: { 1: { k: "Genesis", b: "8:15", e: "8:22", v: 8 }, 7: { k: "Genesis", b: "10:21", e: "10:32", v: 12 }, M: { k: "Genesis", b: "10:26", e: "10:32", v: 7 } },
  triYear: 2 };

const PESACH = { name: { en: "Pesach Shabbat Chol ha-Moed" }, type: "holiday", summary: "Exodus 33:12-34:26; Numbers 28:19-25; Song of Songs 1:1-8:14",
  fullkriyah: { 1: { p: 21, k: "Exodus", b: "33:12", e: "33:16", v: 5 }, 7: { p: 21, k: "Exodus", b: "34:18", e: "34:26", v: 9 }, M: { p: 41, k: "Numbers", b: "28:19", e: "28:25", v: 7 } } };

test("hebcalRef and parseRange: one range in one book, either way round", () => {
  assert.equal(hebcalRef({ k: "Genesis", b: "6:9", e: "6:22" }), "Genesis 6:9-22");
  assert.equal(hebcalRef({ k: "Genesis", b: "7:17", e: "8:14" }), "Genesis 7:17-8:14");
  assert.equal(hebcalRef({ k: "Exodus", b: "30:11", e: "30:11" }), "Exodus 30:11");
  assert.equal(hebcalRef(null), "");
  assert.deepEqual(parseRange("Genesis 6:9-7:16"), { book: "Genesis", from: [6, 9], to: [7, 16] });
  assert.deepEqual(parseRange("I Samuel 20:18-42"), { book: "I Samuel", from: [20, 18], to: [20, 42] });
  assert.deepEqual(parseRange("Exodus 30:11"), { book: "Exodus", from: [30, 11], to: [30, 11] });
  assert.equal(parseRange("Isaiah 27:6-28:13; 29:22-23"), null);
  assert.equal(parseRange("Genesis"), null);
});

test("leyningOf keeps the full aliyot list with the maftir, and the triennial reading with its year", () => {
  const l = leyningOf(NOACH);
  assert.equal(l.parashah, "Noach");
  assert.equal(l.aliyot.length, 8);
  assert.deepEqual(l.aliyot[0], { n: 1, ref: "Genesis 6:9-22", verses: 14 });
  assert.deepEqual(l.aliyot[7], { n: "M", ref: "Genesis 11:29-32", verses: 4 });
  assert.equal(l.triennial.year, 2);

  assert.deepEqual(aliyotOf(CHANUKAH.fullkriyah).at(-1), { n: "M", ref: "Numbers 7:1-17", verses: 17, reason: "Chanukah Day 1 (on Shabbat)" });
});

test("parsePick takes aliyah:N, aliyah:N-M, aliyah:M, triennial and full, and refuses the rest", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(parsePick("aliyah:2"), { kind: "aliyah", from: 2, to: 2 });
  assert.deepEqual(parsePick("aliyah:1-3"), { kind: "aliyah", from: 1, to: 3 });
  assert.deepEqual(parsePick("Aliyah:M"), { kind: "aliyah", from: "M", to: "M" });
  assert.deepEqual(parsePick("triennial"), { kind: "triennial" });
  assert.deepEqual(parsePick("full"), { kind: "full" });
  for (const bad of ["aliyah:0", "aliyah:3-2", "aliyah:8", "aliyah:7-M", "aliyah:M-2", "aliyot:1", "", "first"]) assert.throws(() => parsePick(bad), /--torah-pick/, bad);
});

test("defaultPick: a weekly selection waits for Daniel; a triennial or full custom picks for itself", () => {
  assert.equal(defaultPick({ torah: { mode: "selection", rule: "weekly" } }), null);
  assert.deepEqual(defaultPick({ torah: { mode: "triennial" } }), { kind: "triennial" });
  assert.deepEqual(defaultPick({ torah: { mode: "full" } }), { kind: "full" });
  assert.equal(defaultPick(null), null);
});

test("selectTorah joins aliyot into one range and always carries the maftir beside it", () => {
  const l = leyningOf(NOACH);
  assert.deepEqual(selectTorah(l, parsePick("aliyah:1-2")), { ref: "Genesis 6:9-7:16", book: "Genesis", selection: "aliyot 1-2", maftir: { ref: "Genesis 11:29-32" } });
  assert.equal(selectTorah(l, parsePick("aliyah:3")).ref, "Genesis 7:17-8:14");
  assert.equal(selectTorah(l, parsePick("aliyah:3")).selection, "aliyah 3");
  assert.equal(selectTorah(l, parsePick("full")).ref, "Genesis 6:9-11:32");
  assert.deepEqual(selectTorah(l, parsePick("triennial")), { ref: "Genesis 8:15-10:32", book: "Genesis", selection: "triennial year 2", maftir: { ref: "Genesis 11:29-32" } });
  assert.deepEqual(selectTorah(l, { kind: "ref", ref: "Genesis 6:9-6:22" }), { ref: "Genesis 6:9-6:22", book: "Genesis", selection: "custom", maftir: { ref: "Genesis 11:29-32" } });
  assert.throws(() => selectTorah(l, { kind: "ref", ref: "Genesis 6" }), /is not one range/);
});

test("selectTorah on Shabbat Chanukah: the special maftir is reported, never folded into a Genesis selection", () => {
  const l = leyningOf(CHANUKAH);
  const s = selectTorah(l, parsePick("aliyah:1"));
  assert.equal(s.ref, "Genesis 37:1-11");
  assert.deepEqual(s.maftir, { ref: "Numbers 7:1-17", reason: "Chanukah Day 1 (on Shabbat)" });
  assert.deepEqual(selectTorah(l, parsePick("aliyah:M")), { ref: "Numbers 7:1-17", book: "Numbers", selection: "maftir", maftir: { ref: "Numbers 7:1-17", reason: "Chanukah Day 1 (on Shabbat)" } });
  assert.throws(() => selectTorah(l, parsePick("triennial")), /Hebcal gives no triennial reading for Vayeshev/);
  assert.throws(() => selectTorah(l, parsePick("aliyah:1-2")), /Hebcal lists no aliyah 2 for Vayeshev/, "a gap in the list (this item is trimmed) is refused, never bridged");
  const p = leyningOf(PESACH);
  assert.equal(selectTorah(p, parsePick("full")).ref, "Exodus 33:12-34:26");
  assert.equal(selectTorah(p, parsePick("full")).selection, "full reading");
});

test("aliyotLines and aliyotMarkdown show the whole list, the maftir's reason and the selection (or how to make one)", () => {
  const l = leyningOf(CHANUKAH);
  const lines = aliyotLines(l);
  assert.equal(lines[0], "Torah: Vayeshev (Hebcal)");
  assert.ok(lines.includes("  M  Numbers 7:1-17  (17 verses; Chanukah Day 1 (on Shabbat))"));
  const md = aliyotMarkdown(leyningOf(NOACH), null);
  assert.match(md, /^# Torah calendar: Noach\n/);

  assert.match(md, /Triennial year 2: Genesis 8:15-10:32\./);
  assert.match(md, /Selection: none yet\. Pick with fetch\.mjs --torah-pick aliyah:N\[-M\], triennial or full, or --torah "<ref>"\./);
  assert.match(aliyotMarkdown(leyningOf(NOACH), { ref: "Genesis 6:9-7:16", selection: "aliyot 1-2" }), /Selection: aliyot 1-2, Genesis 6:9-7:16\./);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `scripts/leyning.mjs`**

```js
/**
 * leyning.mjs — the week's Torah reading as Hebcal gives it, and the selection the sheet carries.
 * Pure: fetch.mjs makes the one network call (Hebcal's leyning API, CC BY 4.0) and hands its item here.
 *
 * Hebcal's item has `fullkriyah` (aliyot 1-7 and M, each { k: book, b: begin, e: end, v: verses }),
 * `triennial` (the same shape, with `triYear`) and, on special Shabbatot and festivals, the special
 * maftir with its `reason`. The full list is kept on the sheet so the class sees where the selection
 * sits; the selection is one range in one book; the maftir is reported beside it, never folded in.
 */
const ALIYOT = ["1", "2", "3", "4", "5", "6", "7"];

/** Hebcal's { k, b, e } → "Genesis 6:9-22", "Genesis 7:17-8:14", or "Exodus 30:11" for one verse. */
export function hebcalRef(a) {
  if (!a || !a.k || !a.b) return "";
  const e = a.e || a.b;
  if (e === a.b) return `${a.k} ${a.b}`;
  const bc = a.b.split(":")[0], [ec, ev] = e.split(":");
  return `${a.k} ${a.b}-${bc === ec ? ev : e}`;
}

/** "Genesis 6:9-7:16" → { book, from: [6, 9], to: [7, 16] }; null unless it is one range in one book. */
export function parseRange(ref) {
  // The book has no digits, colons or semicolons ("I Samuel", "Song of Songs"), so a ref of two ranges never parses.
  const m = /^([^:;\d]+?)\s+(\d+):(\d+)(?:-(?:(\d+):)?(\d+))?$/.exec(String(ref == null ? "" : ref).trim());
  if (!m) return null;
  const from = [Number(m[2]), Number(m[3])];
  const to = m[5] ? [m[4] ? Number(m[4]) : from[0], Number(m[5])] : from;
  return { book: m[1], from, to };
}

const formatRange = (book, [c1, v1], [c2, v2]) =>
  c1 === c2 && v1 === v2 ? `${book} ${c1}:${v1}` : c1 === c2 ? `${book} ${c1}:${v1}-${v2}` : `${book} ${c1}:${v1}-${c2}:${v2}`;

/** One Hebcal aliyot set → [{ n, ref, verses?, reason? }], aliyot 1-7 then M. */
export function aliyotOf(set) {
  if (!set) return [];
  const out = [];
  for (const n of [...ALIYOT, "M"]) {
    const a = set[n];
    if (!a) continue;
    const row = { n: n === "M" ? "M" : Number(n), ref: hebcalRef(a) };
    if (a.v != null) row.verses = a.v;
    if (a.reason) row.reason = a.reason;
    out.push(row);
  }
  return out;
}

/** What the sheet keeps of Hebcal's item (sheet.json `leyning`, working data only; not published). */
export function leyningOf(item) {
  return {
    parashah: (item && item.name && item.name.en) || "",
    type: (item && item.type) || "shabbat",
    summary: (item && item.summary) || "",
    aliyot: aliyotOf(item && item.fullkriyah),
    triennial: item && item.triennial ? { year: item.triYear == null ? null : item.triYear, aliyot: aliyotOf(item.triennial) } : null,
    source: "Hebcal",
  };
}

/** "aliyah:2", "aliyah:1-3", "aliyah:M", "triennial", "full" → a pick. */
export function parsePick(text) {
  const t = String(text == null ? "" : text).trim().toLowerCase();
  if (t === "triennial" || t === "full") return { kind: t };
  const usage = `--torah-pick takes aliyah:N, aliyah:N-M (1 to 7), aliyah:M, triennial or full (got "${text}")`;
  const m = /^aliyah:([1-7m])(?:-([1-7]))?$/.exec(t);
  if (!m) throw new Error(usage);
  if (m[1] === "m") { if (m[2]) throw new Error(usage); return { kind: "aliyah", from: "M", to: "M" }; }
  const from = Number(m[1]), to = m[2] ? Number(m[2]) : from;
  if (to < from) throw new Error(usage);
  return { kind: "aliyah", from, to };
}

/** The pick a congregation's customs make without asking. A weekly selection (CRC) waits for Daniel: null. */
export function defaultPick(customs) {
  const mode = customs && customs.torah && customs.torah.mode;
  return mode === "triennial" ? { kind: "triennial" } : mode === "full" ? { kind: "full" } : null;
}

function joinRows(rows, what) {
  const ranges = rows.map(r => parseRange(r.ref));
  if (!ranges.length || ranges.some(x => !x)) throw new Error(`${what}: Hebcal gave a reading this cannot join (${rows.map(r => r.ref).join("; ")}); give --torah "<ref>"`);
  const book = ranges[0].book;
  if (ranges.some(x => x.book !== book)) throw new Error(`${what} spans two books (${rows.map(r => r.ref).join("; ")}); pick within one book or give --torah "<ref>"`);
  return formatRange(book, ranges[0].from, ranges[ranges.length - 1].to);
}

/** The Torah selection: { ref, book, selection, maftir }. `pick` comes from parsePick, or is { kind: "ref", ref }. */
export function selectTorah(leyning, pick) {
  const rows = (leyning && leyning.aliyot) || [];
  const m = rows.find(r => r.n === "M");
  const maftir = m ? (m.reason ? { ref: m.ref, reason: m.reason } : { ref: m.ref }) : null;
  const name = (leyning && leyning.parashah) || "this Shabbat";
  let ref, selection;
  if (pick.kind === "ref") {
    if (!parseRange(pick.ref)) throw new Error(`--torah "${pick.ref}" is not one range like "Genesis 6:9-7:16"`);
    ref = String(pick.ref).trim(); selection = "custom";
  } else if (pick.kind === "aliyah" && pick.from === "M") {
    if (!m) throw new Error(`Hebcal lists no maftir for ${name}`);
    ref = m.ref; selection = "maftir";
  } else if (pick.kind === "aliyah") {
    const sel = [];
    for (let n = pick.from; n <= pick.to; n++) {
      const r = rows.find(x => x.n === n);
      if (!r) throw new Error(`Hebcal lists no aliyah ${n} for ${name}`);
      sel.push(r);
    }
    ref = joinRows(sel, pick.from === pick.to ? `aliyah ${pick.from}` : `aliyot ${pick.from}-${pick.to}`);
    selection = pick.from === pick.to ? `aliyah ${pick.from}` : `aliyot ${pick.from}-${pick.to}`;
  } else if (pick.kind === "triennial") {
    if (!leyning || !leyning.triennial) throw new Error(`Hebcal gives no triennial reading for ${name}`);
    ref = joinRows(leyning.triennial.aliyot.filter(r => r.n !== "M"), "the triennial reading");
    selection = `triennial year ${leyning.triennial.year}`;
  } else if (pick.kind === "full") {
    ref = joinRows(rows.filter(r => r.n !== "M"), "the full reading");
    selection = "full reading";
  } else throw new Error(`unknown Torah pick "${pick.kind}"`);
  return { ref, book: parseRange(ref).book, selection, maftir };
}

const row = r => `${r.verses != null ? `${r.verses} verses` : ""}${r.reason ? `${r.verses != null ? "; " : ""}${r.reason}` : ""}`;

/** The aliyot list for the console, one line each. */
export function aliyotLines(leyning) {
  const out = [`Torah: ${leyning.parashah} (Hebcal)`];
  for (const r of leyning.aliyot) out.push(`  ${String(r.n).padEnd(2)} ${r.ref}${row(r) ? `  (${row(r)})` : ""}`);
  if (leyning.triennial) {
    try { out.push(`  triennial year ${leyning.triennial.year}: ${selectTorah(leyning, { kind: "triennial" }).ref}`); } catch {}
  }
  return out;
}

/** The digest's "Torah calendar" section: the whole list, the triennial range, and the selection or how to make one. */
export function aliyotMarkdown(leyning, torah) {
  const list = leyning.aliyot.map(r => `${r.n} ${r.ref}${r.verses != null || r.reason ? ` (${[r.verses, r.reason].filter(x => x != null).join("; ")})` : ""}`).join(" · ");
  const out = [`# Torah calendar: ${leyning.parashah}`, "", `From Hebcal: ${list}.`];
  if (leyning.triennial) {
    try { out.push(`Triennial year ${leyning.triennial.year}: ${selectTorah(leyning, { kind: "triennial" }).ref}.`); } catch {}
  }
  out.push(torah && torah.ref
    ? `Selection: ${torah.selection || "custom"}, ${torah.ref}.`
    : `Selection: none yet. Pick with fetch.mjs --torah-pick aliyah:N[-M], triennial or full, or --torah "<ref>".`);
  return out.join("\n") + "\n";
}
```

### Task 4: `fetch --torah`: customs, the selection, per-reading texts, schema 3 (Opus)

**Files:**
- Create: `scripts/week-files.mjs`, `tests/fetch.test.mjs`
- Modify: `scripts/fetch.mjs`, `site/app/contract.js`, `tests/customs.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `loadCustoms` (Task 2); `leyningOf`, `parsePick`, `defaultPick`, `selectTorah`, `aliyotLines` (Task 3); `defaultHaftarahFor` (Task 1); `migrate3`; `L.readings`, `L.parashahKey`; the existing `cleanHebrew`, `cleanEnglish`, `flattenVerses`, `incipitHe`, `incipitEn`, `stripMarks`, `slugify`, `planChapter`, `chapterSegments`, `selectLinked`, `pool`, `weeksTaken`, `nextShabbat`, `arg` in `scripts/fetch.mjs`.
- Produces:
  - `site/app/contract.js`: `PDF_FILES = { haftarah: "sheet.pdf", torah: "sheet-torah.pdf" }`.
  - `scripts/week-files.mjs`: `READING_FILES = { haftarah: { candidates: "candidates.json", texts: "texts.json" }, torah: { candidates: "candidates-torah.json", texts: "texts-torah.json" } }`, `HTML_FILES = { haftarah: "sheet.html", torah: "sheet-torah.html" }`, `TROPE_HTML = "sheet-trope.html"`, `PRIOR_FILE = "prior.json"`, and `PDF_FILES` re-exported.
  - `scripts/fetch.mjs`: `withRetry(fn, { delays = [1500, 4000], sleepImpl, retryable })` (three tries); `getJSON(url, { fetchImpl, sleepImpl })` (retries a network error or 5xx, never a 4xx); `leyningUrl(date, customs)`; `fetchPassage(ref, { versions, get = getJSON }) → { ref, heRef, book, verses: [{ ref, book, chapter, verse, he, en, notes, break }] }`; `candidatesFrom(links, { kind }) → { groups, candidates, skipped }` (for `kind: "torah"`, quoting commentaries and super-commentaries are set aside in `skipped`); `TORAH_CORE`; `capToBudget(commentaries, budget) → { kept, dropped }`; `readingFrom(kind, passage, { titleEn, versions, fetched, extra }) → reading`; `freshSheet({ slug, date, item, conv, customs, special, readings, leyning, nextWeek }) → schema-3 sheet`; `mergeWeek(existing, { torah, leyning }) → { sheet, changed, notes }`.
  - CLI: `--customs <id>`, `--torah` (on by default when customs say so), `--torah "<ref>"`, `--torah-pick aliyah:N[-M]|aliyah:M|triennial|full`, `--no-torah`. A kept `sheet.json` gains a Torah reading without `--force` (and is migrated to schema 3 to hold it); a kept Torah reading with entries is never replaced without `--force`.

Decision recorded here: the haftarah keeps its file names (`candidates.json`, `texts.json`), so deliver, digest, the drafting brief and every week already in Drive stay as they are; the Torah gets `candidates-torah.json` and `texts-torah.json` (the spec's `texts-haftarah.json` name is not introduced).

```js
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { withRetry, getJSON, fetchPassage, candidatesFrom, capToBudget, readingFrom, freshSheet, mergeWeek, leyningUrl, TORAH_CORE } from "../scripts/fetch.mjs";
import { makeWeekTorah } from "./helpers/makeWeekTorah.mjs";

const min = JSON.parse(await readFile(new URL("./fixtures/week-min/sheet.json", import.meta.url), "utf8"));
const CUSTOMS = { id: "test", calendar: { diaspora: true }, haftarahRite: "ashkenazi", torah: { mode: "selection" }, versions: { he: "Hebrew Version", en: "English Version" }, entryCap: 24, series: { name: "Test Series" }, credits: { issuedBy: "A Congregation" } };

test("withRetry tries three times with growing waits, and gives up at once on what retrying cannot fix", async () => {
  const waits = [];
  let n = 0;
  assert.equal(await withRetry(async () => { if (++n < 3) throw new Error("503"); return "ok"; }, { sleepImpl: async ms => waits.push(ms) }), "ok");
  assert.deepEqual(waits, [1500, 4000]);
  n = 0;
  await assert.rejects(withRetry(async () => { n++; throw new Error("always"); }, { sleepImpl: async () => {} }), /always/);
  assert.equal(n, 3);
  n = 0;
  await assert.rejects(withRetry(async () => { n++; throw new Error("404"); }, { sleepImpl: async () => {}, retryable: e => e.message !== "404" }), /404/);
  assert.equal(n, 1);
});

test("getJSON retries a 5xx and a network error, never a 4xx", async () => {
  let calls = 0;
  const flaky = async () => (++calls < 3 ? new Response("busy", { status: 503, statusText: "Busy" }) : new Response('{"ok":1}', { status: 200 }));
  assert.deepEqual(await getJSON("https://example.test/x", { fetchImpl: flaky, sleepImpl: async () => {} }), { ok: 1 });
  assert.equal(calls, 3);
  calls = 0;
  const missing = async () => { calls++; return new Response("no", { status: 404, statusText: "Not Found" }); };
  await assert.rejects(getJSON("https://example.test/y", { fetchImpl: missing, sleepImpl: async () => {} }), /404 Not Found for https:\/\/example\.test\/y/);
  assert.equal(calls, 1);
});

test("leyningUrl asks Hebcal for the customs' calendar", () => {
  assert.equal(leyningUrl("2026-10-17", CUSTOMS), "https://www.hebcal.com/leyning?cfg=json&start=2026-10-17&end=2026-10-17&i=off");
  assert.match(leyningUrl("2026-10-17", { ...CUSTOMS, calendar: { diaspora: false } }), /&i=on$/);
});

test("fetchPassage asks for the customs' versions and gives every verse its book", async () => {
  const urls = [];
  const get = async url => { urls.push(url); return {
    book: "Genesis", heRef: "synthetic", sections: [6, 9], toSections: [6, 11],
    versions: [{ languageFamilyName: "hebrew", text: ["אבג דהו", "זחט", "יכל"] }, { languageFamilyName: "english", text: ["One here.", "Two here.", "Three here."] }],
  }; };
  const p = await fetchPassage("Genesis 6:9-11", { versions: CUSTOMS.versions, get });
  assert.ok(urls[0].includes(`version=hebrew|${encodeURIComponent("Hebrew Version")}`) && urls[0].includes(`version=english|${encodeURIComponent("English Version")}`));
  assert.equal(p.book, "Genesis");
  assert.deepEqual(p.verses.map(v => [v.ref, v.book, v.chapter, v.verse]), [["Genesis 6:9", "Genesis", 6, 9], ["Genesis 6:10", "Genesis", 6, 10], ["Genesis 6:11", "Genesis", 6, 11]]);
  await assert.rejects(fetchPassage("Genesis 6:9-11", { versions: CUSTOMS.versions, get: async () => ({ versions: [] }) }), /did not return both versions for Genesis 6:9-11/);
});

test("candidatesFrom sets super-commentaries and quoting commentary aside for the Torah only", () => {
  const links = [
    { index_title: "Rashi on Genesis", category: "Commentary", ref: "Rashi on Genesis 6:9:1", anchorRef: "Genesis 6:9", sourceHasEn: true },
    { index_title: "Siftei Chakhamim", category: "Commentary", ref: "Siftei Chakhamim, Genesis 6:9:1", anchorRef: "Genesis 6:9" },
    { index_title: "Mechokekei Yehudah; Yahel Ohr", category: "Commentary", ref: "Mechokekei Yehudah, Genesis 6:9", anchorRef: "Genesis 6:9" },
    { index_title: "Some Later Work", category: "Quoting Commentary", ref: "Some Later Work 3", anchorRef: "Genesis 6:9" },
  ];
  const t = candidatesFrom(links, { kind: "torah" });
  assert.deepEqual(t.candidates.map(c => c.source), ["Rashi on Genesis"]);
  assert.deepEqual(t.skipped.map(s => s.source).sort(), ["Mechokekei Yehudah; Yahel Ohr", "Siftei Chakhamim", "Some Later Work"]);
  const h = candidatesFrom(links, { kind: "haftarah" });
  assert.equal(h.candidates.length, 4);
  assert.deepEqual(h.skipped, []);
  assert.ok(TORAH_CORE.includes("Ramban") && TORAH_CORE.includes("Rashbam"));
});

test("capToBudget drops whole commentaries from the end, never the first", () => {
  const big = n => ({ segments: { s: "x".repeat(n) } });
  const { kept, dropped } = capToBudget({ "Rashi on Genesis": big(300), "Ramban on Genesis": big(300), "Sforno on Genesis": big(300) }, 800);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(kept), ["Rashi on Genesis", "Ramban on Genesis"]);
  assert.deepEqual(dropped, ["Sforno on Genesis"]);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(capToBudget({ "Rashi on Genesis": big(5000) }, 10).kept), ["Rashi on Genesis"]);
});

test("freshSheet writes schema 3: Torah first, then the haftarah; series, versions and credits from the customs", () => {
  const passage = { ref: "Genesis 6:9-11", heRef: null, book: "Genesis", verses: [{ ref: "Genesis 6:9", book: "Genesis", chapter: 6, verse: 9, he: "אבג דהו", en: "One here, and more.", notes: [], break: null }] };
  const torah = readingFrom("torah", passage, { titleEn: "Noach", versions: CUSTOMS.versions, fetched: "t", extra: { selection: "aliyah 1", aliyot: [], maftir: null } });
  const haftarah = readingFrom("haftarah", { ...passage, ref: "Isaiah 54:1", book: "Isaiah" }, { titleEn: "Haftarat Noach", versions: CUSTOMS.versions, fetched: "t", extra: { defaultHaftarah: null } });
  const s = freshSheet({ slug: "2026-10-17-noach", date: "2026-10-17", item: { name: { en: "Noach", he: "נֹחַ" }, summary: "Genesis 6:9-11:32" }, conv: { hebrew: "ו׳ חשון", hd: 6, hm: "Cheshvan", hy: 5787 }, customs: CUSTOMS, special: null, readings: [torah, haftarah], leyning: { parashah: "Noach", aliyot: [] }, nextWeek: null });
  assert.equal(s.schema, 3);
  assert.deepEqual(s.readings.map(r => [r.id, r.kind]), [["torah", "torah"], ["haftarah", "haftarah"]]);
  assert.deepEqual(s.series, { name: "Test Series", year: "5787" });
  assert.deepEqual(s.credits, { issuedBy: "A Congregation" });
  assert.deepEqual(s.readings[0].versions, CUSTOMS.versions);
  assert.equal(s.readings[0].selection, "aliyah 1");
  assert.equal(s.shabbat.hebrewEn, "6 Cheshvan 5787");
  assert.deepEqual(s.leyning, { parashah: "Noach", aliyot: [] });
  assert.ok(!("haftarah" in s) && !("verses" in s));
});

const TORAH = { id: "torah", kind: "torah", ref: "Genesis 6:9-22", book: "Genesis", selection: "aliyah 1", verses: [{ book: "Genesis", chapter: 6, verse: 9 }], glosses: [], commentary: [] };
const LEY = { parashah: "Noach", aliyot: [{ n: 1, ref: "Genesis 6:9-22" }] };

test("mergeWeek: a kept schema-2 week without a Torah pick is left exactly as it was", () => {
  const r = mergeWeek(min, { torah: null, leyning: LEY });
  assert.equal(r.sheet, min);
  assert.equal(r.changed, false);
});

test("mergeWeek: a Torah pick on a kept schema-2 week migrates it and puts the Torah first; the haftarah keeps every entry", () => {
  const r = mergeWeek(min, { torah: TORAH, leyning: LEY });
  assert.equal(r.changed, true);
  assert.equal(r.sheet.schema, 3);
  assert.deepEqual(r.sheet.readings.map(x => x.id), ["torah", "haftarah"]);
  assert.equal(r.sheet.readings[1].commentary.length, min.commentary.length);
  assert.ok(r.sheet.readings[1].commentary.every(e => /^e-[0-9a-f]{8}/.test(e.id)));
  assert.deepEqual(r.sheet.leyning, LEY);
  assert.deepEqual(r.notes, ["added the Torah reading Genesis 6:9-22"]);
  assert.equal(min.schema, 2, "the input is not changed");
});

test("mergeWeek: the same pick again changes nothing; an untouched Torah reading is replaced; one with entries is kept", () => {
  const once = mergeWeek(min, { torah: TORAH, leyning: LEY }).sheet;
  const again = mergeWeek(once, { torah: TORAH, leyning: LEY });
  assert.equal(again.changed, false);
  const other = { ...TORAH, ref: "Genesis 7:1-16", selection: "aliyah 2" };
  const swapped = mergeWeek(once, { torah: other, leyning: LEY });
  assert.equal(swapped.sheet.readings[0].ref, "Genesis 7:1-16");
  assert.deepEqual(swapped.notes, ["replaced the Torah reading Genesis 6:9-22 with Genesis 7:1-16"]);
  const worked = makeWeekTorah(min);   // its Torah reading has an entry and a gloss
  assert.throws(() => mergeWeek(worked, { torah: other, leyning: LEY }), /Torah reading \(Genesis 6:9-11\) already has 1 entry and 1 gloss; --force replaces the whole sheet/);
});
```
