with

```js
const HEADERS = { accept: "application/json", "user-agent": "crc-haftarah-sheet/1.0" };
const wait = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));

/** fn(attempt), tried up to delays.length + 1 times; an error `retryable` refuses is thrown at once. */
export async function withRetry(fn, { delays = [1500, 4000], sleepImpl = wait, retryable = () => true } = {}) {
  for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
    try { return await fn(attempt); }
    catch (err) {
      if (attempt >= delays.length || !retryable(err)) throw err;
      await sleepImpl(delays[attempt]);
    }
  }
}

/** GET JSON; a network error or a 5xx is retried, a 4xx is not. */
export async function getJSON(url, { fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch, sleepImpl = wait } = {}) {
  return withRetry(async () => {
    const res = await fetchImpl(url, { headers: HEADERS });
    if (!res.ok) { const e = new Error(`${res.status} ${res.statusText} for ${url}`); e.status = res.status; throw e; }
    return res.json();
  }, { sleepImpl, retryable: e => !(e.status >= 400 && e.status < 500) });
}

/** Hebcal's leyning item for one date, on the customs' calendar (Diaspora: i=off). */
export const leyningUrl = (date, customs) => `https://www.hebcal.com/leyning?cfg=json&start=${date}&end=${date}&i=${customs.calendar.diaspora ? "off" : "on"}`;
```

After the `export const CORE_COMMENTATORS = …;` line add:

```js
// The Torah's core list (torah-and-library.md section 3): the Torah draws five to seven times the
// commentary a haftarah does, so only these are fetched by chapter, in this order, and the last ones
// give way first when the file would pass its budget. Titles Sefaria lacks as "<X> on <Book>" are
// listed in notFetched, as for the haftarah.
export const TORAH_CORE = ["Rashi", "Ramban", "Ibn Ezra", "Sforno", "Rashbam", "Or HaChaim", "Kli Yakar", "Chizkuni", "Shadal", "Steinsaltz", "Haamek Davar", "Rabbeinu Bahya", "Bekhor Shor"];
// Set aside from the Torah's candidates: glosses on Rashi and the like, and quoting commentaries,
// which sort to the top by link count and bury the commentators (Mechokekei Yehudah alone: 646 links on Noach).
const SUPER_COMMENTARY = /^(Siftei Chakhamim|Mizrachi|Gur Aryeh|Divrei David|Be'er BaSadeh|Mechokekei Yehudah|Yahel Ohr|Nachalat Yaakov|Levush HaOrah|Imrei Shefer)/;
```

Replace the whole `async function sefariaText(ref) { … }` with:

```js
async function sefariaText(ref, { fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch, sleepImpl = wait } = {}) {
  const url = `https://www.sefaria.org/api/v3/texts/${encodeURIComponent(ref)}?version=hebrew&version=${encodeURIComponent("english|all")}&return_format=default`;
  try {
    return await withRetry(async () => {
      const res = await fetchImpl(url, { headers: HEADERS });
      if (res.status === 404 || res.status === 400) return null;
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(String(res.status));
      const j = await res.json();
      return j && !j.error && Array.isArray(j.versions) && j.versions.length ? j : null;
    }, { sleepImpl });
  } catch { return null; }
}
```

Delete the line `const sleep = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));` (now `wait`, above).

- [ ] **Step 5: fetch.mjs, the texts.** Replace the whole `async function buildTexts({ haftRef, book, verses, links, groups }) { … }` with:

```js
/** Drop whole commentaries from the end (the least central first) until the set fits `budget` bytes as written; the first always stays. */
export function capToBudget(commentaries, budget = TEXTS_BUDGET) {
  const kept = { ...commentaries }, dropped = [];
  const order = Object.keys(kept);
  while (order.length > 1 && Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(kept, null, 1)) > budget) { const k = order.pop(); delete kept[k]; dropped.unshift(k); }
  return { kept, dropped };
}

async function buildTexts({ ref, book, verses, links, groups, core = CORE_COMMENTATORS, extras = true, capCommentaries = false }) {
  const chapters = new Map();
  for (const v of verses) { if (!chapters.has(v.chapter)) chapters.set(v.chapter, new Set()); chapters.get(v.chapter).add(v.verse); }

  // Core commentators, plus (for a haftarah) any other "<X> on <Book>" commentary linked to three or more verses.
  const indexes = core.map(c => `${c} on ${book}`);
  if (extras) for (const g of groups.values()) {
    if (g.category === "Commentary" && g.source.endsWith(` on ${book}`) && !/^Sources and References/.test(g.source) && g.verses.size >= 3 && !indexes.includes(g.source)) indexes.push(g.source);
  }
  const jobs = indexes.flatMap(index => [...chapters.keys()].map(ch => ({ index, ch })));
  const results = await pool(jobs, 4, async ({ index, ch }) => ({ index, ch, tx: await sefariaText(`${index} ${ch}`) }));

  let commentaries = {};
  const missing = [];
  for (const { index, ch, tx } of results) {
    if (!tx || tx.indexTitle !== index) { if (!missing.includes(index)) missing.push(index); continue; }
    const { segments, coverage, heVersion, enVersions } = chapterSegments(tx, book, ch, chapters.get(ch));
    const c = commentaries[index] ||= { index, core: core.some(n => index === `${n} on ${book}`), versions: { he: heVersion, en: [] }, segments: {}, coverage: {} };
    for (const e of enVersions || []) if (!c.versions.en.includes(e)) c.versions.en.push(e);
    Object.assign(c.segments, segments);
    Object.assign(c.coverage, coverage);
  }
  for (const [k, c] of Object.entries(commentaries)) if (!Object.keys(c.segments).length) { delete commentaries[k]; missing.push(k); }
  const missingNow = missing.filter(m => !commentaries[m]);
  let overBudgetCommentaries = [];
  if (capCommentaries) ({ kept: commentaries, dropped: overBudgetCommentaries } = capToBudget(commentaries));

  // Linked texts.
  const { selected, skipped } = selectLinked(links, new Set(Object.keys(commentaries)));
  const fetched = await pool(selected, 4, async e => ({ e, tx: await sefariaText(e.ref) }));
  let bytes = Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(commentaries, null, 1));
  const linked = {};
  const overBudget = [], failed = [];
  for (const { e, tx } of fetched) {
    if (!tx) { failed.push(e.ref); continue; }
    const heV = tx.versions.find(v => v.languageFamilyName === "hebrew");
    const en = tx.versions.filter(v => v.languageFamilyName === "english").map(v => ({ versionTitle: v.versionTitle, text: Array.isArray(v.text) ? v.text : rtrim(v.text) })).filter(v => textLen(v.text));
    const entry = { source: e.source, category: e.category, kind: e.kind, anchors: e.anchors, he: heV ? (Array.isArray(heV.text) ? heV.text : rtrim(heV.text)) : "", heVersion: heV?.versionTitle || null, en };
    const size = Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify({ [e.ref]: entry }, null, 1));
    if (bytes + size > TEXTS_BUDGET) { overBudget.push(e.ref); continue; }
    bytes += size;
    linked[e.ref] = entry;
  }

  const notFetched = {
    commentaries: missingNow,
    linkedOverCap: skipped.map(e => ({ ref: e.ref, source: e.source, category: e.category, kind: e.kind, anchors: e.anchors })),
    linkedOverBudget: overBudget,
    linkedFailed: failed,
  };
  if (capCommentaries) notFetched.commentariesOverBudget = overBudgetCommentaries;
  return { ref, fetched: new Date().toISOString(), verses: verses.map(v => `${book} ${v.chapter}:${v.verse}`), commentaries, linked, notFetched };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: fetch.mjs, the passage, the candidates, the sheet.** Replace everything from the line `// ---- main -------------------------------------------------------------------` down to (not including) the final `if (process.argv[1] && fileURLToPath(import.meta.url) === resolve(process.argv[1])) {` guard with:

```js
// ---- one reading ------------------------------------------------------------------

/** One passage, verse-aligned in the customs' two versions; every verse names its book (schema 3). */
export async function fetchPassage(ref, { versions, get = getJSON }) {
  const refPath = encodeURIComponent(ref.replace(/\s+/g, " "));
  const tx = await get(`https://www.sefaria.org/api/v3/texts/${refPath}?version=hebrew|${encodeURIComponent(versions.he)}&version=english|${encodeURIComponent(versions.en)}&return_format=default`);
  const heV = (tx.versions || []).find(v => v.languageFamilyName === "hebrew");
  const enV = (tx.versions || []).find(v => v.languageFamilyName === "english");
  if (!heV || !enV) throw new Error("Sefaria did not return both versions for " + ref);
  const heVerses = flattenVerses(heV.text, tx.sections, tx.toSections);
  const enVerses = flattenVerses(enV.text, tx.sections, tx.toSections);
  const book = tx.book || tx.indexTitle || ref.replace(/\s+\d.*$/, "");
  const verses = heVerses.map((hv, i) => {
    const { he, break: brk } = cleanHebrew(hv.text || "");
    const { en, notes } = cleanEnglish((enVerses[i] && enVerses[i].text) || "");
    return { ref: `${book} ${hv.chapter}:${hv.verse}`, book, chapter: hv.chapter, verse: hv.verse, he, en, notes, break: brk };
  });
  return { ref, heRef: tx.heRef || null, book, verses };
}

const sefariaLinks = (ref, get = getJSON) => get(`https://www.sefaria.org/api/links/${encodeURIComponent(ref.replace(/\s+/g, " "))}?with_text=0`);

/** Sefaria's links → sources grouped for the rabbi to choose from. For the Torah, quoting commentaries and super-commentaries are set aside in `skipped`. */
export function candidatesFrom(links, { kind = "haftarah" } = {}) {
  const groups = new Map(), skipped = new Map();
  for (const l of links) {
    const title = l.index_title || l.collectiveTitle?.en || l.ref.replace(/\s+\d.*$/, "");
    if (kind === "torah" && (l.category === "Quoting Commentary" || SUPER_COMMENTARY.test(title))) {
      const s = skipped.get(title) || { source: title, category: l.category, count: 0 };
      s.count++; skipped.set(title, s);
      continue;
    }
    const g = groups.get(title) || { source: title, category: l.category, type: l.type, count: 0, verses: new Set(), hasEnglish: false, refs: [] };
    g.count++;
    g.hasEnglish = g.hasEnglish || !!l.sourceHasEn;
    for (const v of (l.anchorRefExpanded || [l.anchorRef])) g.verses.add(v);
    if (g.refs.length < 60) g.refs.push(l.ref);
    groups.set(title, g);
  }
  const candidates = [...groups.values()].map(g => {
    const register = g.category === "Reference" ? "reference" : (REGISTERS.find(([re]) => re.test(g.source)) || [null, g.category === "Commentary" || g.category === "Targum" || g.category === "Talmud" || g.category === "Midrash" || g.category === "Chasidut" || g.category === "Kabbalah" ? "traditional" : "unclassified"])[1];
    return { ...g, register, verses: [...g.verses].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, undefined, { numeric: true })) };
  }).sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count);
  return { groups, candidates, skipped: [...skipped.values()].sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count) };
}

/** A reading of the sheet from its passage: the incipit fetch proposes, the customs' versions, no entries yet. */
export function readingFrom(kind, passage, { titleEn, versions, fetched, extra = {} }) {
  const first = passage.verses[0] || { he: "", en: "" };
  return {
    id: kind, kind, ref: passage.ref, heRef: passage.heRef, book: passage.book, ...extra,
    incipit: { he: incipitHe(first.he), en: incipitEn(first.en) },
    title: { he: stripMarks(incipitHe(first.he)), en: titleEn },
    versions: { he: versions.he, en: versions.en }, fetched,
    verses: passage.verses, glosses: [], commentary: [],
  };
}

/** A new week's sheet.json, schema 3. */
export function freshSheet({ slug, date, item, conv, customs, special = null, readings, leyning = null, nextWeek = null }) {
  const sheet = {
    schema: 3,
    slug,
    status: "draft",
    shabbat: {
      civil: date,
      civilDisplay: new Date(date + "T12:00:00Z").toLocaleDateString("en-US", { year: "numeric", month: "long", day: "numeric", timeZone: "UTC" }),
      hebrew: conv.hebrew,
      hebrewEn: `${conv.hd} ${conv.hm} ${conv.hy}`,
      parashah: { en: item.name?.en, he: item.name?.he, ref: item.summary },
      special,
    },
    series: { name: customs.series.name, year: String(conv.hy) },
    readings,
    openingNote: { calendar: "", setting: [] },
    glossary: [],
    nextWeek,
    credits: { ...(customs.credits || {}) },
  };
  if (leyning) sheet.leyning = leyning;
  return sheet;
}

const count = (n, one, many) => `${n} ${n === 1 ? one : many}`;

/**
 * A kept sheet.json and this run's Torah reading and aliyot. A schema-2 week with no Torah pick is
 * returned untouched. A Torah pick is added first in readings[] (a schema-2 week is migrated to hold
 * it); the same pick again changes nothing; a Torah reading nobody has worked on yet is replaced by a
 * new pick; one with entries or glosses is never replaced here.
 */
export function mergeWeek(existing, { torah = null, leyning = null } = {}) {
  const notes = [];
  const has = L.readings(existing).find(r => r.kind === "torah") || null;
  if (torah && has && has.ref === torah.ref) torah = null;
  if (torah && has && ((has.commentary || []).length || (has.glosses || []).length)) {
    throw new Error(`sheet.json's Torah reading (${has.ref}) already has ${count((has.commentary || []).length, "entry", "entries")} and ${count((has.glosses || []).length, "gloss", "glosses")}; --force replaces the whole sheet, or change readings[] by hand`);
  }
  const holdsLeyning = torah || Number(existing.schema) >= 3;
  const newLeyning = leyning && holdsLeyning && JSON.stringify(existing.leyning) !== JSON.stringify(leyning);
  if (!torah && !newLeyning) return { sheet: existing, changed: false, notes };
  const sheet = Number(existing.schema) >= 3 ? structuredClone(existing) : migrate3(existing);
  if (newLeyning) sheet.leyning = leyning;
  if (torah) {
    sheet.readings = [torah, ...(sheet.readings || []).filter(r => r.kind !== "torah")];
    notes.push(has ? `replaced the Torah reading ${has.ref} with ${torah.ref}` : `added the Torah reading ${torah.ref}`);
  }
  return { sheet, changed: true, notes };
}

// ---- main -------------------------------------------------------------------

// The following Shabbat's reading; Daniel confirms it when the calendar offers a choice.
async function nextWeekFor(date, customs) {
  const d = new Date(date + "T12:00:00Z"); d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + 7);
  const next = d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  try {
    const ley = await getJSON(leyningUrl(next, customs));
    const it = (ley.items || [])[0];
    if (!it || !it.haftara) return null;
    return {
      shabbat: it.name?.en || "",
      special: it.reason?.haftara || null,
      ref: it.haftara,
      civilDisplay: new Date(next + "T12:00:00Z").toLocaleDateString("en-US", { year: "numeric", month: "long", day: "numeric", timeZone: "UTC" }),
    };
  } catch { return null; }
}

// Week folders already made for this Shabbat: local weeks/ plus --known slugs (the Drive working
// folder's subfolders, which a fresh cloud session cannot see).
export function weeksTaken(date, local = [], known = []) {
  return [...new Set([...local, ...known])].filter(d => d.startsWith(date)).sort();
}

async function main() {
  const customsArg = arg("customs", null);
  const customs = await loadCustoms(typeof customsArg === "string" ? customsArg : "crc");
  const date = arg("date", nextShabbat());
  const outRoot = arg("out", "weeks");
  const force = arg("force", false) === true;
  const refOverride = arg("ref", null);
  const known = String(arg("known", "") === true ? "" : arg("known", "")).split(",").map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const wantTexts = !process.argv.includes("--no-texts");
  const noTorah = process.argv.includes("--no-torah");
  const torahArg = arg("torah", null), pickArg = arg("torah-pick", null);
  if (noTorah && (typeof torahArg === "string" || pickArg)) throw new Error("--no-torah cannot be combined with --torah <ref> or --torah-pick");
  const pick = typeof torahArg === "string" ? { kind: "ref", ref: torahArg } : typeof pickArg === "string" ? parsePick(pickArg) : defaultPick(customs);
  if ((customs.toConfirm || []).length) console.log(`customs/${customs.id}.json: not yet confirmed by Daniel: ${customs.toConfirm.join(", ")}`);
  const hebcal = d => getJSON(leyningUrl(d, customs));

  // "This week" is ambiguous once the coming Shabbat already has a folder: name both Shabbatot and
  // stop, so the question to Daniel can offer the one after as well.
  if (arg("date", null) === null) {
    const taken = weeksTaken(date, await readdir(outRoot).catch(() => []), known);
    if (taken.length) {
      const after = new Date(date + "T12:00:00Z"); after.setUTCDate(after.getUTCDate() + 7);
      const next = after.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      const name = async d => { try { const it = (await hebcal(d)).items?.[0]; return it ? `${it.name?.en}, haftarah ${it.haftara}` : "?"; } catch { return "?"; } };
      const local = new Set(await readdir(outRoot).catch(() => []));
      console.log(`${date} already has ${taken.map(t => local.has(t) ? `${outRoot}/${t}` : `${t} (Drive)`).join(", ")}.`);
      console.log(`  ${date}: ${await name(date)}\n  ${next}: ${await name(next)}`);
      console.log(`Run again with --date ${date} or --date ${next}.`);
      process.exit(2);
    }
  }

  console.log(`Shabbat ${date}`);

  // 1. Calendar: Hebcal's leyning item (parashah, haftarah, aliyot, the reason when special) and the Hebrew date.
  const item = ((await hebcal(date)).items || [])[0];
  if (!item) throw new Error(`Hebcal has no reading for ${date}; is it a Shabbat?`);
  const conv = await getJSON(`https://www.hebcal.com/converter?cfg=json&gy=${date.slice(0, 4)}&gm=${+date.slice(5, 7)}&gd=${+date.slice(8, 10)}&g2h=1`);
  const name = item.name?.en || "";
  const haftRef = refOverride || item.haftara;
  if (!haftRef) throw new Error(`No haftarah listed for ${date} (${name}).`);
  const special = item.reason?.haftara || null;
  const defaultHaftarah = defaultHaftarahFor(name, customs.haftarahRite);
  const leyning = noTorah ? null : leyningOf(item);
  console.log(`Parashat ${name}; haftarah ${haftRef}${special ? ` (${special})` : ""}`);
  if (leyning) console.log(aliyotLines(leyning).join("\n"));
  const fetched = new Date().toISOString();

  // 2. The haftarah, verse-aligned in both versions.
  const hp = await fetchPassage(haftRef, { versions: customs.versions });
  const haftarah = readingFrom("haftarah", hp, {
    titleEn: special ? special.replace(/^Shabbat\s+/i, "") : `Haftarat ${name}`, versions: customs.versions, fetched,
    extra: { defaultHaftarah: special && defaultHaftarah !== haftRef ? defaultHaftarah : null },
  });

  // 3. The Torah selection, when there is one: Daniel's pick, or the customs' standing rule.
  let torah = null;
  if (leyning && pick) {
    const sel = selectTorah(leyning, pick);
    const tp = await fetchPassage(sel.ref, { versions: customs.versions });
    torah = readingFrom("torah", tp, { titleEn: name, versions: customs.versions, fetched, extra: { selection: sel.selection, aliyot: leyning.aliyot, maftir: sel.maftir } });
    console.log(`Torah selection: ${sel.selection}, ${sel.ref} (${tp.verses.length} verses)${sel.maftir ? `; maftir ${sel.maftir.ref}${sel.maftir.reason ? ` (${sel.maftir.reason})` : ""}` : ""}`);
  } else if (leyning) {
    console.log(`Torah: no selection yet. Put the aliyot to Daniel in the briefing, then run again with --torah-pick aliyah:N[-M] (or aliyah:M, triennial, full) or --torah "<ref>".`);
  }

  // 4. sheet.json: a new week, or the kept one, with a Torah reading added when there is a new pick.
  const slug = `${date}-${slugify(name || "shabbat")}${special ? "-" + slugify(special.replace(/^Shabbat\s+/i, "")) : ""}`;
  const dir = join(outRoot, slug);
  await mkdir(dir, { recursive: true });
  const sheetPath = join(dir, "sheet.json");
  const existing = await readFile(sheetPath, "utf8").then(JSON.parse, () => null);
  let sheet, write = null;
  if (!existing || force) {
    sheet = freshSheet({ slug, date, item, conv, customs, special, readings: [torah, haftarah].filter(Boolean), leyning, nextWeek: await nextWeekFor(date, customs) });
    write = existing ? "Replaced" : "Wrote";
  } else {
    const m = mergeWeek(existing, { torah, leyning });
    for (const n of m.notes) console.log(`  ${n}`);
    sheet = m.sheet;
    if (m.changed) write = "Updated";
  }

  // 5. Write sheet.json.
  if (write) {
    await writeFile(sheetPath, JSON.stringify(sheet, null, 2), "utf8");
    console.log(`${write} ${sheetPath} (${L.readings(sheet).map(r => `${r.id}: ${(r.verses || []).length} verses`).join("; ")})`);
  } else console.log(`Kept existing ${sheetPath} (use --force to replace). Refreshed candidates and texts only.`);

  // 6. Candidates and texts, one pair of files per reading the sheet holds.
  for (const r of L.readings(sheet).filter(x => (x.verses || []).length && READING_FILES[x.kind])) {
    const files = READING_FILES[r.kind];
    const links = await sefariaLinks(r.ref);
    const { groups, candidates, skipped } = candidatesFrom(links, { kind: r.kind });
    const cand = { ref: r.ref, fetched, sources: candidates };
    if (r.kind === "torah") cand.skipped = skipped;
    await writeFile(join(dir, files.candidates), JSON.stringify(cand, null, 2), "utf8");
    console.log(`Wrote ${join(dir, files.candidates)} (${candidates.length} sources, ${links.length} links${skipped.length ? `; ${skipped.length} super-commentaries and quoting commentaries set aside` : ""})`);
    if (!wantTexts) continue;
    const opts = r.kind === "torah" ? { core: TORAH_CORE, extras: false, capCommentaries: true } : {};
    const texts = await buildTexts({ ref: r.ref, book: r.book, verses: r.verses, links, groups, ...opts });
    const body = JSON.stringify(texts, null, 1);
    await writeFile(join(dir, files.texts), body, "utf8");
    const segs = Object.values(texts.commentaries).reduce((s, c) => s + Object.keys(c.segments).length, 0);
    const sf = Object.values(texts.commentaries).flatMap(c => Object.entries(c.segments).filter(([, x]) => x.sectionFiled).map(([ref]) => ref));
    console.log(`Wrote ${join(dir, files.texts)} (${(Buffer.byteLength(body) / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB: ${Object.keys(texts.commentaries).length} commentaries, ${segs} segments, ${Object.keys(texts.linked).length} linked texts)`);
    if (sf.length) console.log(`  section-filed: ${[...new Set(sf.map(x => x.replace(/:\d+$/, "")))].join(", ")}`);
    const nf = texts.notFetched;
    if (nf.commentaries.length) console.log(`  not on Sefaria: ${nf.commentaries.join(", ")}`);
    if ((nf.commentariesOverBudget || []).length) console.log(`  over the size budget, not fetched: ${nf.commentariesOverBudget.join(", ")}`);
    if (nf.linkedOverCap.length + nf.linkedOverBudget.length + nf.linkedFailed.length) console.log(`  linked not fetched: ${nf.linkedOverCap.length} over cap, ${nf.linkedOverBudget.length} over size budget, ${nf.linkedFailed.length} failed`);
  }
  if (wantTexts) console.log(`  overview: node scripts/digest.mjs ${dir}`);
  console.log(`\nNext: review candidates, add entries to sheet.json, then\n  node scripts/build.mjs ${dir} --png`);
}
```

Replace `digestInput` (Task 1's version) with:

```js
// What the digest needs from sheet.json for one reading: the book and the verse refs, read through
// readingView so a schema-3 week gives the same answer as its schema-2 original. It never throws: a
// reading the sheet does not hold, or a sheet with none, says nothing, as no sheet does, and the
// digest falls back to texts.json's own verses.
export function digestInput(sheet, readingId = null) {
  let view = null;
  try {
    if (sheet && (!readingId || L.readings(sheet).some(r => r.id === readingId))) view = L.readingView(sheet, readingId || undefined);
  } catch { view = null; }
  return { book: view?.haftarah?.book, verses: (view?.verses || []).map(v => v.ref).filter(Boolean) };
}

/** The week's digest.md: the Torah calendar when the sheet has one, then a digest per reading, Torah first. */
export function weekDigest({ sheet, readings }) {
  const parts = [];
  if (sheet && sheet.leyning) parts.push(aliyotMarkdown(sheet.leyning, L.readings(sheet).find(r => r.kind === "torah") || null));
  for (const kind of ["torah", "haftarah"]) {
    const x = readings[kind];
    if (!x || (!x.texts && !x.candidates)) continue;
    parts.push(buildDigest({ texts: x.texts, candidates: x.candidates, ...digestInput(sheet, kind) }));
  }
  return parts.join("\n");
}
```

```js
async function main() {
  const dir = process.argv[2];
  if (!dir) { console.error("usage: node scripts/digest.mjs weeks/<slug>"); process.exit(1); }
  const sheet = await readJSON(join(dir, "sheet.json"));
  const readings = {};
  for (const kind of ["torah", "haftarah"]) {
    const f = READING_FILES[kind];
    readings[kind] = { texts: await readJSON(join(dir, f.texts)), candidates: await readJSON(join(dir, f.candidates)) };
  }
  if (!Object.values(readings).some(x => x.texts || x.candidates)) { console.error(`No texts.json or candidates.json in ${dir}; run fetch.mjs first.`); process.exit(1); }
  const md = weekDigest({ sheet, readings });
  await writeFile(join(dir, "digest.md"), md, "utf8");
  process.stdout.write(md);
}
```

Replace the module-level `let weekDir, WANT_PDF, WANT_PNG, WANT_TROPE;` and `parseArgs()` with:

```js
let weekDir, WANT_PDF, WANT_PNG, WANT_TROPE, READING, CUSTOMS;
function parseArgs() {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  const valueOf = name => { const i = args.indexOf(`--${name}`); return i < 0 ? null : args[i + 1]; };
  const dirArg = args.find((a, i) => !a.startsWith("--") && !["--reading", "--customs"].includes(args[i - 1]));
  if (!dirArg) { console.error("usage: node scripts/build.mjs weeks/<slug> [--reading torah|haftarah] [--png] [--no-pdf] [--trope] [--customs <id>]"); process.exit(1); }
  weekDir = resolve(dirArg);
  WANT_PDF = !flag("no-pdf");
  WANT_PNG = flag("png");
  WANT_TROPE = flag("trope");
  READING = valueOf("reading");
  if (READING && !["torah", "haftarah"].includes(READING)) { console.error(`--reading takes torah or haftarah (got ${READING})`); process.exit(1); }
  CUSTOMS = valueOf("customs") || "crc";
}

/** The readings this build makes: every reading with verses, in the sheet's order, or only the one --reading names. */
export function readingsToBuild(file, only = null) {
  const all = HaftarahLib.readings(file).filter(r => (r.verses || []).length);
  const pick = only ? all.filter(r => r.kind === only) : all;
  if (!pick.length) throw new Error(only ? `this sheet has no ${only} reading with verses` : "this sheet has no reading with verses");
  return pick.map(r => ({ id: r.id, kind: r.kind }));
}
```

Replace the whole `async function main() { … }` with:

```js
async function main() {
  parseArgs();
  const file = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(weekDir, "sheet.json"), "utf8"));
  let targets;
  try { targets = readingsToBuild(file, READING); } catch (err) { console.error(`Not built: ${err.message}`); process.exit(1); }
  const multi = HaftarahLib.readings(file).length > 1;

  // The citation gate for every reading built first: nothing is written while any of them fails.
  const builds = [], problems = [];
  for (const t of targets) {
    // One reading in the schema-2 shape; a schema-2 file is itself, so its sheet.html does not change.
    const data = HaftarahLib.readingView(file, t.id);
    let biblio = null;
    try { biblio = HaftarahLib.parseBibliography(await readFile(join(ROOT, "bibliography", `${data.haftarah.book}.md`), "utf8")); } catch {}
    problems.push(...gate(data, biblio).map(p => (multi ? `${t.id}: ${p}` : p)));
    builds.push({ ...t, data });
  }
  if (problems.length) { console.error("Not built; fix these first:\n" + problems.map(x => "  - " + x).join("\n")); process.exit(1); }

  // Entry lint (scripts/lint.mjs): errors stop the build, warnings print and the build goes on.
  const voices = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(ROOT, "template", "voices.json"), "utf8"));
  const linted = lintSheet(file, { voices, customs: await loadCustoms(CUSTOMS), only: targets.map(t => t.id) });
  if (linted.warnings.length) console.warn(`Lint warnings:\n${formatLint(linted.warnings)}`);
  if (linted.errors.length) {
    const fixable = linted.fixes.length ? "\n(node scripts/lint.mjs <week> --fix rewrites gloss lemmas to the verse's exact text)" : "";
    console.error(`Not built; fix these first:\n${formatLint(linted.errors)}${fixable}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  const logo = await findLogo();
  const tpl = await readFile(join(ROOT, "template", "sheet.html"), "utf8");
  // The chanter's copy is the same page with trope: true in its options and its own title; the
  // Torah sheet is the same page with reading: "torah".
  const pageHtml = (data, options, titleText) => {
    const shown = (data.commentary || []).filter(e => e.status !== "rejected").length;
    return fillTemplate(tpl, {
      TITLE: titleText.replace(/[<>&]/g, ""),
      CSS: toPosix(relative(weekDir, join(ROOT, "template", "sheet.css"))),
      RENDER: toPosix(relative(weekDir, join(ROOT, "template", "render.js"))),
      LIB: toPosix(relative(weekDir, join(ROOT, "template", "lib.js"))),
      DATA: JSON.stringify(data).replace(/<\/script/gi, "<\\/script"),
      OPTIONS: JSON.stringify(options),
      NOTE: `${shown} commentary entr${shown === 1 ? "y" : "ies"}.`,
    });
  };

  const tropePath = join(weekDir, TROPE_HTML);
  for (const b of builds) {
    const torah = b.kind === "torah";
    const title = `${b.data.shabbat?.parashah?.en || ""} ${torah ? "Torah" : "haftarah"}`;
    b.html = join(weekDir, HTML_FILES[b.kind]);
    await writeFile(b.html, pageHtml(b.data, torah ? { logo, voices, reading: "torah" } : { logo, voices }, title), "utf8");
    console.log(`Wrote ${relative(ROOT, b.html)}${logo ? ` (logo: ${logo})` : " (no logo in assets/; typographic wordmark)"}`);
    if (torah) continue;
    if (WANT_TROPE) {
      await writeFile(tropePath, pageHtml(b.data, { logo, voices, trope: true }, `${title} (chanter’s copy)`), "utf8");
      console.log(`Wrote ${relative(ROOT, tropePath)}`);
    } else if (existsSync(join(weekDir, "sheet-trope.pdf"))) {
      // A chanter's copy left from an earlier build no longer matches this sheet; say so rather than delete it.
      console.warn(`${relative(ROOT, join(weekDir, "sheet-trope.pdf"))} is from an earlier build; --trope rebuilds it`);
    }
  }
  if (WANT_TROPE && !builds.some(b => b.kind === "haftarah")) console.warn("--trope makes the haftarah's chanter's copy; this build has no haftarah");

  if (!WANT_PDF && !WANT_PNG) return;

  const browser = findBrowser();
  if (!browser) {
    console.warn("No Chrome or Edge found. Open the HTML in a browser and print to PDF (letter, no margins, background graphics on), or set HAFTARAH_BROWSER.");
    return;
  }
  console.log(`Browser: ${browser}`);
  const profile = join(tmpdir(), `haftarah-chrome-${process.pid}`);
  await mkdir(profile, { recursive: true });
  const common = chromeFlags(profile);
  const kb = buf => `${Math.round(buf.length / 1024)} KB`;

  try {
    for (const b of builds) {
      // The page images are rendered from the PDF when pdftoppm is present: a headless screenshot
      // on Linux Chromium loses the bottom of the page, and the PDF is what prints.
      const url = pathToFileURL(b.html).href;
      const pdfPath = WANT_PDF ? join(weekDir, PDF_FILES[b.kind]) : join(profile, `probe-${b.kind}.pdf`);
      const { pages, buf } = await printPdf(browser, common, url, pdfPath);
      if (WANT_PDF) console.log(`Wrote ${relative(ROOT, pdfPath)}: ${pages} pages, ${kb(buf)}`);
      if (WANT_PNG) await writePreviews({ browser, common, url, pdfPath, pages, outDir: join(weekDir, b.kind === "torah" ? "preview-torah" : "preview") });
      // The chanter's copy prints after the study sheet, the same way; previews stay the sheet's.
      if (b.kind === "haftarah" && WANT_TROPE && WANT_PDF) {
        const tropePdf = join(weekDir, "sheet-trope.pdf");
        const t = await printPdf(browser, common, pathToFileURL(tropePath).href, tropePdf);
        console.log(`Wrote ${relative(ROOT, tropePdf)}: ${t.pages} pages, ${kb(t.buf)}`);
      }
    }
  } finally {
    await rm(profile, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
  }
}

// Page images of one PDF: pdftoppm at 192 dpi when present, else one headless screenshot per page.
async function writePreviews({ browser, common, url, pdfPath, pages, outDir }) {
  await rm(outDir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
  await mkdir(outDir, { recursive: true });
  const pdftoppm = findPdftoppm();
  if (pdftoppm) {
    // 192 dpi = 2x CSS pixels, 1632 x 2112 px, the same size as the screenshots.
    await run(pdftoppm, ["-png", "-r", "192", pdfPath, join(outDir, "page")]);
    console.log(`Wrote ${pages} page image${pages === 1 ? "" : "s"} to ${relative(ROOT, outDir)}/ (pdftoppm)`);
    return;
  }
  for (let p = 1; p <= pages; p++) {
    const png = join(outDir, `page-${String(p).padStart(2, "0")}.png`);
    await run(browser, [...common, "--window-size=816,1056", "--force-device-scale-factor=2", `--screenshot=${png}`, `${url}?page=${p}`]);
  }
  console.log(`Wrote ${pages} page image${pages === 1 ? "" : "s"} to ${relative(ROOT, outDir)}/ (screenshots; check the PDF if a page looks cut off)`);
}
```

```
 *   node scripts/check.mjs weeks/<slug>                  # every built reading: sheet.html, and sheet-torah.html when the week has a Torah reading
 *   node scripts/check.mjs weeks/<slug> --reading torah  # one reading only
 *   node scripts/check.mjs weeks/<slug> --trope          # the haftarah's chanter's copy, sheet-trope.html
```

Replace `async function main() { … }` with:

```js
async function main() {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  const dirArg = args.find((a, i) => !a.startsWith("--") && args[i - 1] !== "--reading");
  if (!dirArg) { console.error("usage: node scripts/check.mjs weeks/<slug> [--reading torah|haftarah] [--trope]"); process.exit(1); }
  const weekDir = resolve(dirArg);
  const trope = args.includes("--trope");
  const ri = args.indexOf("--reading");
  const only = ri >= 0 ? args[ri + 1] : null;
  const file = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(weekDir, "sheet.json"), "utf8"));
  // The chanter's copy exists for the haftarah only.
  const targets = trope
    ? [{ id: L.primaryReading(file).id, kind: "haftarah", html: TROPE_HTML }]
    : L.readings(file).filter(r => (r.verses || []).length && (!only || r.kind === only)).map(r => ({ id: r.id, kind: r.kind, html: HTML_FILES[r.kind] }));
  if (!targets.length) { console.error(`${dirArg}: no ${only || ""} reading with verses to check`); process.exit(1); }
  const browser = findBrowser();
  if (!browser) { console.error("No Chrome or Edge found."); process.exit(1); }
  console.log(`Browser: ${browser}`);
  let failed = false;
  for (const t of targets) {
    const html = join(weekDir, t.html);
    // A missing file would render as Chrome's error page and surface as a page-count mismatch.
    if (!(await access(html).then(() => true, () => false))) {
      console.error(`${dirArg.replace(/[\\/]+$/, "")}/${t.html} is missing; run node scripts/build.mjs ${dirArg}${trope ? " --trope" : ""} first`);
      failed = true;
      continue;
    }
    const url = pathToFileURL(html).href;
    const a = analyse(await dumpDom(browser, url));
    const b = analyse(await dumpDom(browser, url));
    const problems = a.problems.slice();
    if (JSON.stringify(a.summary) !== JSON.stringify(b.summary)) problems.push("two renders differ (pagination is not deterministic on this browser)");
    // The divine-name count in the render must equal the count in the data (verses plus incipit).
    const data = L.readingView(file, t.id);
    const yyExpected = [...data.verses.map(v => v.he), data.haftarah.incipit?.he || ""].reduce((n, h) => n + (String(h).match(TETRA) || []).length, 0);
    if (yyExpected !== a.summary.yy) problems.push(`divine name: ${a.summary.yy} replaced in the render, ${yyExpected} in the data`);
    if (targets.length > 1) console.log(`\n${t.kind === "torah" ? "Torah" : "Haftarah"}: ${t.html}`);
    console.log(`Pages: ${a.summary.pages}; divine name ${a.summary.yy}x; keys ${a.summary.keys.length}; voices without a line: ${a.summary.voicesMissing.join(", ") || "none"}`);
    console.table(a.summary.page.map((p, i) => ({ page: i + 1, kind: p.kind, rows: p.rows, gap: p.gap, fill: p.fill, split: p.split, offpage: p.offpage, voices: p.voices || "" })));
    if (problems.length) {
      console.error("Check failed:\n  - " + problems.join("\n  - "));
      if (a.diagnostics.length) console.error("\nGap diagnostics:\n" + a.diagnostics.join("\n"));
      failed = true;
    }
  }
  if (failed) process.exit(1);
  console.log("Check passed.");
}
```

- Modify: `scripts/deliver.mjs`, `scripts/publish.mjs`, `tests/deliver.test.mjs`, `tests/publish.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PDF_FILES`, `READING_FILES`, `PRIOR_FILE` (Task 4); `makeWeekTorah` (Task 2); `HAFTARAH_RITES` (Task 1, in a test); `L.readings`, `L.readingView`, `L.primaryReading`, `L.parashahKey`.

  - `site/app/parashot.js`: `TORAH_BOOKS`, `PARASHOT` (`[{ key, en, he, book, order }]`, 54 rows in Torah order), `COMBINED` (`{ "vayakhel-pekudei": ["vayakhel", "pekudei"], … }`, 7 pairs), `PROPHET_BOOKS` (21), `SPECIALS` (`[{ tag, en, re }]`), `keyOf(name)` (lib.js's `parashahKey`, for the browser), `isParashahKey(key)`, `parashahKeysOf(name) → string[]` (both halves for a combined week; a festival's own key), `parashahName(key)`, `weekKeys(indexRow) → string[]`, `weekTags({ parashah, special }) → string[]`.

  - `scripts/publish.mjs`: `publish()` uploads `sheet-torah.pdf` (after `sheet.pdf`, before the index) when the week has a Torah reading and refuses without it; `indexEntry` adds `parashahKeys`, `tags`, `pdfTorah` (path or null) and `selection` on a Torah reading's row.

```js
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import { PARASHOT, COMBINED, TORAH_BOOKS, PROPHET_BOOKS, SPECIALS, keyOf, isParashahKey, parashahKeysOf, parashahName, weekKeys, weekTags } from "../site/app/parashot.js";
import { HAFTARAH_RITES } from "../scripts/rites.mjs";
const L = createRequire(import.meta.url)("../template/lib.js");

test("54 parashot in Torah order, five books, unique keys", () => {
  assert.equal(PARASHOT.length, 54);
  assert.deepEqual(PARASHOT.map(p => p.order), Array.from({ length: 54 }, (_, i) => i + 1));
  assert.equal(new Set(PARASHOT.map(p => p.key)).size, 54);
  assert.deepEqual(TORAH_BOOKS.map(b => PARASHOT.filter(p => p.book === b).length), [12, 11, 10, 10, 11]);
  assert.equal(PARASHOT[0].key, "bereshit"); assert.equal(PARASHOT[53].key, "vezot-haberakhah");
  assert.ok(PARASHOT.every(p => p.he && /[א-ת]/.test(p.he)));
  assert.equal(Object.keys(COMBINED).length, 7);
  for (const [k, pair] of Object.entries(COMBINED)) assert.ok(pair.every(isParashahKey) && k === pair.join("-"), k);
  assert.equal(PROPHET_BOOKS.length, 21);
});

test("keys are lib.js's parashahKey of Hebcal's names, so a key made in the browser matches one made by publish", () => {
  for (const name of ["Lech-Lecha", "Ha'azinu", "Sh'lach", "Beha'alotcha", "Re'eh", "Vayakhel-Pekudei", "Achrei Mot-Kedoshim", "Pesach Shabbat Chol ha-Moed", "I Samuel"]) assert.equal(keyOf(name), L.parashahKey(name), name);
  for (const p of PARASHOT) assert.equal(keyOf(p.en), p.key, p.en);
  assert.ok(Object.keys(HAFTARAH_RITES.ashkenazi).every(isParashahKey), "the rite table and the library agree on every key");
});

test("parashahKeysOf: both halves of a combined parashah; a single one; a festival's own key", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(parashahKeysOf("Vayakhel-Pekudei"), ["vayakhel", "pekudei"]);
  assert.deepEqual(parashahKeysOf("Lech-Lecha"), ["lech-lecha"]);
  assert.deepEqual(parashahKeysOf("Pesach Shabbat Chol ha-Moed"), ["pesach-shabbat-chol-ha-moed"]);
  assert.deepEqual(parashahKeysOf(""), []);
  assert.equal(parashahName("matot-masei"), "Matot-Masei");
  assert.equal(parashahName("haazinu"), "Ha'azinu");
  assert.deepEqual(weekKeys({ parashahKeys: ["matot", "masei"] }), ["matot", "masei"]);
  assert.deepEqual(weekKeys({ parashahKey: "matot-masei" }), ["matot", "masei"], "a row written before parashahKeys");
  assert.deepEqual(weekKeys({ parashah: "Noach" }), ["noach"]);
});

test("weekTags files special Shabbatot and festivals by Hebcal's reason or name", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(weekTags({ parashah: "Vayeshev", special: "Chanukah Day 1 (on Shabbat)" }), ["chanukah"]);
  assert.deepEqual(weekTags({ parashah: "Ha'azinu", special: "Shabbat Shuva (with Ha'azinu)" }), ["shuva"]);
  assert.deepEqual(weekTags({ parashah: "Bereshit", special: "Shabbat Machar Chodesh" }), ["machar-chodesh"]);
  assert.deepEqual(weekTags({ parashah: "Vaera", special: "Shabbat Rosh Chodesh" }), ["rosh-chodesh"]);
  assert.deepEqual(weekTags({ parashah: "Miketz", special: "Shabbat Rosh Chodesh Chanukah" }), ["chanukah", "rosh-chodesh"]);
  assert.deepEqual(weekTags({ parashah: "Vayakhel", special: "Shabbat Shekalim" }), ["shekalim"]);
  assert.deepEqual(weekTags({ parashah: "Pesach Shabbat Chol ha-Moed", special: null }), ["pesach"]);
  assert.deepEqual(weekTags({ parashah: "Noach", special: null }), []);
  assert.ok(SPECIALS.every(s => /^[a-z-]+$/.test(s.tag) && s.en));
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `site/app/parashot.js`**

```js
// The library's one table: the 54 parashot in Torah order (key, English, Hebrew, book), the seven
// that are sometimes read together, the books, and the special Shabbatot and festivals the library
// groups by. publish.mjs files each week under it (parashahKeys, tags); the landing's views and the
// vercel.json alias allow-list are made from it, and a test holds vercel.json to it. Keys are lib.js's
// parashahKey of Hebcal's English names; keyOf is the same function, for pages that load no lib.js.
export const TORAH_BOOKS = ["Genesis", "Exodus", "Leviticus", "Numbers", "Deuteronomy"];

export const PARASHOT = [
  ["bereshit", "Bereshit", "בראשית", "Genesis"], ["noach", "Noach", "נח", "Genesis"], ["lech-lecha", "Lech-Lecha", "לך לך", "Genesis"],
  ["vayera", "Vayera", "וירא", "Genesis"], ["chayei-sara", "Chayei Sara", "חיי שרה", "Genesis"], ["toldot", "Toldot", "תולדות", "Genesis"],
  ["vayetzei", "Vayetzei", "ויצא", "Genesis"], ["vayishlach", "Vayishlach", "וישלח", "Genesis"], ["vayeshev", "Vayeshev", "וישב", "Genesis"],
  ["miketz", "Miketz", "מקץ", "Genesis"], ["vayigash", "Vayigash", "ויגש", "Genesis"], ["vayechi", "Vayechi", "ויחי", "Genesis"],
  ["shemot", "Shemot", "שמות", "Exodus"], ["vaera", "Vaera", "וארא", "Exodus"], ["bo", "Bo", "בא", "Exodus"],
  ["beshalach", "Beshalach", "בשלח", "Exodus"], ["yitro", "Yitro", "יתרו", "Exodus"], ["mishpatim", "Mishpatim", "משפטים", "Exodus"],
  ["terumah", "Terumah", "תרומה", "Exodus"], ["tetzaveh", "Tetzaveh", "תצוה", "Exodus"], ["ki-tisa", "Ki Tisa", "כי תשא", "Exodus"],
  ["vayakhel", "Vayakhel", "ויקהל", "Exodus"], ["pekudei", "Pekudei", "פקודי", "Exodus"],
  ["vayikra", "Vayikra", "ויקרא", "Leviticus"], ["tzav", "Tzav", "צו", "Leviticus"], ["shmini", "Shmini", "שמיני", "Leviticus"],
  ["tazria", "Tazria", "תזריע", "Leviticus"], ["metzora", "Metzora", "מצרע", "Leviticus"], ["achrei-mot", "Achrei Mot", "אחרי מות", "Leviticus"],
  ["kedoshim", "Kedoshim", "קדשים", "Leviticus"], ["emor", "Emor", "אמר", "Leviticus"], ["behar", "Behar", "בהר", "Leviticus"],
  ["bechukotai", "Bechukotai", "בחקתי", "Leviticus"],
  ["bamidbar", "Bamidbar", "במדבר", "Numbers"], ["nasso", "Nasso", "נשא", "Numbers"], ["behaalotcha", "Beha'alotcha", "בהעלתך", "Numbers"],
  ["shlach", "Sh'lach", "שלח", "Numbers"], ["korach", "Korach", "קרח", "Numbers"], ["chukat", "Chukat", "חקת", "Numbers"],
  ["balak", "Balak", "בלק", "Numbers"], ["pinchas", "Pinchas", "פינחס", "Numbers"], ["matot", "Matot", "מטות", "Numbers"],
  ["masei", "Masei", "מסעי", "Numbers"],
  ["devarim", "Devarim", "דברים", "Deuteronomy"], ["vaetchanan", "Vaetchanan", "ואתחנן", "Deuteronomy"], ["eikev", "Eikev", "עקב", "Deuteronomy"],
  ["reeh", "Re'eh", "ראה", "Deuteronomy"], ["shoftim", "Shoftim", "שפטים", "Deuteronomy"], ["ki-teitzei", "Ki Teitzei", "כי תצא", "Deuteronomy"],
  ["ki-tavo", "Ki Tavo", "כי תבוא", "Deuteronomy"], ["nitzavim", "Nitzavim", "נצבים", "Deuteronomy"], ["vayeilech", "Vayeilech", "וילך", "Deuteronomy"],
  ["haazinu", "Ha'azinu", "האזינו", "Deuteronomy"], ["vezot-haberakhah", "Vezot Haberakhah", "וזאת הברכה", "Deuteronomy"],
].map(([key, en, he, book], i) => ({ key, en, he, book, order: i + 1 }));

export const COMBINED = {
  "vayakhel-pekudei": ["vayakhel", "pekudei"], "tazria-metzora": ["tazria", "metzora"], "achrei-mot-kedoshim": ["achrei-mot", "kedoshim"],
  "behar-bechukotai": ["behar", "bechukotai"], "chukat-balak": ["chukat", "balak"], "matot-masei": ["matot", "masei"], "nitzavim-vayeilech": ["nitzavim", "vayeilech"],
};

export const PROPHET_BOOKS = ["Joshua", "Judges", "I Samuel", "II Samuel", "I Kings", "II Kings", "Isaiah", "Jeremiah", "Ezekiel",
  "Hosea", "Joel", "Amos", "Obadiah", "Jonah", "Micah", "Nahum", "Habakkuk", "Zephaniah", "Haggai", "Zechariah", "Malachi"];

// Special Shabbatot and festivals, matched against Hebcal's haftarah reason ("Shabbat Shuva (with
// Ha'azinu)", "Chanukah Day 1 (on Shabbat)") or a festival week's own name ("Pesach Shabbat Chol ha-Moed").
export const SPECIALS = [
  { tag: "shuva", en: "Shabbat Shuvah", re: /\bShuva/i },
  { tag: "chazon", en: "Shabbat Chazon", re: /\bChazon\b/i },
  { tag: "nachamu", en: "Shabbat Nachamu", re: /\bNachamu\b/i },
  { tag: "shekalim", en: "Shabbat Shekalim", re: /\bShekalim\b/i },
  { tag: "zachor", en: "Shabbat Zachor", re: /\bZachor\b/i },
  { tag: "parah", en: "Shabbat Parah", re: /\bParah\b/i },
  { tag: "hachodesh", en: "Shabbat HaChodesh", re: /\bHaChodesh\b/i },
  { tag: "hagadol", en: "Shabbat HaGadol", re: /\bHaGadol\b/i },
  { tag: "chanukah", en: "Shabbat Chanukah", re: /\bChanukah\b/i },
  { tag: "rosh-chodesh", en: "Shabbat Rosh Chodesh", re: /\bRosh Chodesh\b/i },
  { tag: "machar-chodesh", en: "Shabbat Machar Chodesh", re: /\bMachar Chodesh\b/i },
  { tag: "rosh-hashana", en: "Rosh Hashanah", re: /\bRosh Hashana/i },
  { tag: "yom-kippur", en: "Yom Kippur", re: /\bYom Kippur\b/i },
  { tag: "sukkot", en: "Sukkot", re: /\bSukkot\b/i },
  { tag: "shmini-atzeret", en: "Shemini Atzeret", re: /\bShmini Atzeret\b/i },
  { tag: "pesach", en: "Pesach", re: /\bPesach\b/i },
  { tag: "shavuot", en: "Shavuot", re: /\bShavuot\b/i },
];

/** lib.js's parashahKey: "Ha'azinu" → "haazinu", "I Samuel" → "i-samuel". */
export const keyOf = name => String(name == null ? "" : name).toLowerCase().replace(/['’]/g, "").replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "");
export const isParashahKey = key => PARASHOT.some(p => p.key === key) || Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(COMBINED, key);

/** The parashot a week's name covers: both halves of a combined week, one, or a festival's own key. */
export function parashahKeysOf(name) {
  const k = keyOf(name);
  if (!k) return [];
  return COMBINED[k] ? COMBINED[k].slice() : [k];
}

export function parashahName(key) {
  const p = PARASHOT.find(x => x.key === key);
  if (p) return p.en;
  return COMBINED[key] ? COMBINED[key].map(parashahName).join("-") : key;
}

/** An index row's parashah keys; rows written before parashahKeys existed fall back to parashahKey, then the name. */
export function weekKeys(row) {
  if (row && Array.isArray(row.parashahKeys) && row.parashahKeys.length) return row.parashahKeys;
  if (row && row.parashahKey) return parashahKeysOf(row.parashahKey);
  return parashahKeysOf((row && row.parashah) || "");
}

/** The special Shabbatot and festivals a week belongs to, in SPECIALS order. */
export function weekTags({ parashah = "", special = null } = {}) {
  const festival = isParashahKey(keyOf(parashah)) ? "" : parashah;
  const text = `${special || ""} ${festival || ""}`;
  return SPECIALS.filter(s => s.re.test(text)).map(s => s.tag);
}
```

```js
const hasTorah = sheet => L.readings(sheet).some(r => r.kind === "torah" && (r.verses || []).length);

/** One row of index.json (v2: the v1 fields, plus what the library groups by). */
export function indexEntry(sheet, slug, published) {
  const s = sheet.shabbat || {}, p = s.parashah || {}, h = L.primaryReading(sheet) || {};
  return {
    slug, civil: s.civil || "", civilDisplay: s.civilDisplay || "", hebrew: s.hebrew || "", hebrewEn: s.hebrewEn || "",
    shabbat: L.shabbatName(sheet), ref: L.enDash(h.ref || ""), book: h.book || "",
    incipit: { he: (h.incipit && h.incipit.he) || "", en: (h.incipit && h.incipit.en) || "" },
    defaultHaftarah: h.defaultHaftarah ? L.enDash(h.defaultHaftarah) : null,
    pdf: weekPath(slug, PDF_FILES.haftarah), published,
    parashah: p.en || "", parashahHe: p.he || "", parashahKey: L.parashahKey(p.en || ""),
    cycle: String((sheet.series && sheet.series.year) || (s.hebrewEn || "").split(/\s+/).pop() || ""),
    special: s.special || null,
    readings: L.readings(sheet).map(r => ({ id: r.id, kind: r.kind, ref: L.enDash(r.ref || ""), book: r.book || "", ...(r.selection ? { selection: r.selection } : {}) })),
    parashahKeys: parashahKeysOf(p.en || ""),
    tags: weekTags({ parashah: p.en || "", special: s.special || null }),
    pdfTorah: hasTorah(sheet) ? weekPath(slug, PDF_FILES.torah) : null,
  };
}
```

```js
/** "2026-10-17 Noach — Isaiah 54.1-55.5.pdf"; with a label (a week with two readings), "2026-10-17 Noach Haftarah — …". */
export function publicName(sheet, { label = null } = {}) {
  const date = sheet?.shabbat?.civil;
  const parashah = sheet?.shabbat?.parashah?.en;
  const ref = sheet?.haftarah?.ref;
  if (!date || !parashah || !ref) {
    throw new Error("sheet.json needs shabbat.civil, shabbat.parashah.en and haftarah.ref to name the PDF");
  }
  const clean = s => String(s).replace(/:/g, ".").replace(/[\/\\]/g, "-").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  return `${clean(date)} ${clean(parashah)}${label ? ` ${label}` : ""} — ${clean(ref)}.pdf`;
}
```

### Task 8: Carry-forward, on by default: "Last time", `prior.json`, carried entries, "See also", `--no-carry` (Sonnet)

**Files:**
- Create: `scripts/prior.mjs`, `tests/prior.test.mjs`
- Modify: `scripts/fetch.mjs`, `scripts/digest.mjs`, `scripts/lint.mjs`, `scripts/publish.mjs`, `tests/digest.test.mjs`, `tests/lint.test.mjs`, `README.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `weekKeys`, `isParashahKey`, `parashahKeysOf` from `site/app/parashot.js` (Task 7); `indexUrl`, `dataUrl` from `site/app/contract.js`; `PRIOR_FILE` (Task 4); `migrate3`; `weekDigest` (Task 5); `L.readings`, `L.assignEntryIds`, `L.parashahKey`.
- Produces (`scripts/prior.mjs`):
  - `SITE = "https://haftarah.centralreform.org"` (where the public library is read; GET only)
  - `findPrior(index, { keys, civil }) → indexRow | null`: the newest week before `civil` sharing a parashah key (a combined week matches either half; among same-day sheets the plainer slug wins)
  - `priorFromSheet(sheet, { slug, source }) → Prior = { slug, source, hebrewEn, cycle, parashahKey, readings: [{ id, kind, ref, book, entries, glosses, cut }] }`: entries are the printed ones with their ids (a schema-2 sheet's get the ids `migrate --to 3` would give) and their chapter filled in; `cut` lists rejected entries (only a Drive folder has them)
  - `noPrior(keys, error?) → { slug: null, parashahKey, searched, error? }`
  - `lookupPrior({ keys, civil, priorDir = null, fetchImpl, site = SITE }) → Promise<Prior | noPrior>`: `priorDir` (a Drive-restored week folder) first; else the public `index.json` and that week's `sheet.json`; an unreachable library gives `noPrior` with the reason, never a throw
  - `carryInto(sheet, prior) → { sheet, added: [{ reading, id, chapter, verse, source }] }`: copies each prior entry whose verse is in this year's reading of the same kind and book, with its id, `status: "carried"` and `carried: "<prior slug>"`, skipping ids already present (rerunning adds nothing)
  - `seeAlsoSentence(prior) → "See also the 5786 sheet: haftarah.centralreform.org/5786/noach."` (a festival's prior, with no parashah alias, links its slug)
  - `withSeeAlso(openingNote, sentence) → openingNote` (appends to the calendar paragraph once)
  - `applyPrior(sheet, prior, { carry = true, fresh = false }) → { sheet, changed, added }`: carry-forward is on by default (Daniel's ruling: prior entries carry forward and he cuts). With a prior it sets `sheet.prior = { slug, hebrewEn, cycle }`, carries the fitting entries and adds the sentence, on a fresh sheet and on a kept one (a rerun adds nothing: ids already present, a cut entry stays `rejected`). With `carry: false` (`--no-carry`) only a fresh sheet gets the pointer, and a kept sheet is untouched
  - `lastTimeMarkdown(prior, sheet) → markdown` (the digest's "Last time" section)
- Also: `fetch.mjs` `--prior <dir>`, `--no-carry`, and `prior.json` written every run; `weekDigest({ sheet, readings, prior })`; lint warns on each carried entry; `PUBLISHED_FIELDS` gains `prior` (a pointer only, never the entries).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** Create `tests/prior.test.mjs`:

```js
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFile, mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { findPrior, priorFromSheet, noPrior, lookupPrior, carryInto, seeAlsoSentence, withSeeAlso, applyPrior, lastTimeMarkdown } from "../scripts/prior.mjs";
import { migrate3 } from "../scripts/migrate.mjs";
import { makeWeekTorah } from "./helpers/makeWeekTorah.mjs";

const load = async f => JSON.parse(await readFile(new URL(`./fixtures/${f}/sheet.json`, import.meta.url), "utf8"));
const row = (slug, civil, extra = {}) => ({ slug, civil, cycle: civil.slice(0, 4) === "2025" ? "5786" : "5787", ...extra });

test("findPrior: the newest earlier week with a shared key; a later week or another parashah never", () => {
  const index = { weeks: [
    row("2026-10-17-noach", "2026-10-17", { parashahKeys: ["noach"] }),
    row("2025-10-25-noach", "2025-10-25", { parashahKeys: ["noach"] }),
    row("2024-11-02-noach", "2024-11-02", { parashahKeys: ["noach"] }),
    row("2025-11-01-lech-lecha", "2025-11-01", { parashahKeys: ["lech-lecha"] }),
  ] };
  assert.equal(findPrior(index, { keys: ["noach"], civil: "2026-10-17" }).slug, "2025-10-25-noach");
  assert.equal(findPrior(index, { keys: ["noach"], civil: "2024-11-02" }), null);
  assert.equal(findPrior({ weeks: [] }, { keys: ["noach"], civil: "2026-10-17" }), null);
  assert.equal(findPrior(null, { keys: ["noach"], civil: "2026-10-17" }), null);
});

test("findPrior: a combined week is last time for either half, and either half for the combined week", () => {
  const index = { weeks: [row("2025-03-22-vayakhel-pekudei", "2025-03-22", { parashahKeys: ["vayakhel", "pekudei"] }), row("2024-03-09-vayakhel", "2024-03-09", { parashahKeys: ["vayakhel"] })] };
  assert.equal(findPrior(index, { keys: ["vayakhel"], civil: "2027-03-06" }).slug, "2025-03-22-vayakhel-pekudei");
  assert.equal(findPrior(index, { keys: ["pekudei"], civil: "2027-03-13" }).slug, "2025-03-22-vayakhel-pekudei");
  assert.equal(findPrior(index, { keys: ["vayakhel", "pekudei"], civil: "2025-03-21" }).slug, "2024-03-09-vayakhel");
  const legacy = { weeks: [{ slug: "2025-10-25-noach", civil: "2025-10-25", parashah: "Noach" }] };
  assert.equal(findPrior(legacy, { keys: ["noach"], civil: "2026-10-17" }).slug, "2025-10-25-noach", "a row from before parashahKeys still matches by name");
});

test("priorFromSheet: a schema-2 sheet's printed entries with the ids migrate would give, chapters filled, cuts listed", async () => {
  const notes = await load("week-notes");
  notes.commentary[2].status = "rejected";
  const p = priorFromSheet(notes, { slug: "2025-10-04-bereshit-machar-chodesh", source: "folder" });
  assert.equal(p.cycle, "5787");
  assert.equal(p.parashahKey, "bereshit");
  const [r] = p.readings;
  assert.deepEqual([r.id, r.kind, r.book], ["haftarah", "haftarah", "I Samuel"]);
  assert.equal(r.entries.length, notes.commentary.length - 1);
  assert.equal(r.cut.length, 1);
  const ids = migrate3(notes).readings[0].commentary.map(e => e.id);
  assert.ok(r.entries.every(e => ids.includes(e.id)), "the same ids migrate --to 3 gives");
  assert.ok(r.entries.every(e => e.chapter === 20 && !("status" in e)));
});

test("carryInto copies what fits this year's reading, marks it carried, and adds nothing on a rerun", async () => {
  const min = await load("week-min");
  const prior = priorFromSheet(makeWeekTorah(min), { slug: "2025-10-25-noach", source: "web library" });
  const fresh = migrate3({ ...min, commentary: [] });
  fresh.readings.unshift({ id: "torah", kind: "torah", ref: "Genesis 6:9-10", book: "Genesis", verses: [{ book: "Genesis", chapter: 6, verse: 9 }, { book: "Genesis", chapter: 6, verse: 10 }], glosses: [], commentary: [] });
  const { sheet, added } = carryInto(fresh, prior);
  assert.deepEqual(added.map(a => [a.reading, `${a.chapter}:${a.verse}`]), [["torah", "6:9"], ["haftarah", "20:18"], ["haftarah", "20:19"]]);
  const carried = sheet.readings[1].commentary;
  assert.ok(carried.every(e => e.status === "carried" && e.carried === "2025-10-25-noach" && /^e-/.test(e.id)));
  assert.equal(fresh.readings[1].commentary.length, 0, "the input is not changed");
  assert.deepEqual(carryInto(sheet, prior).added, []);
  const otherBook = migrate3({ ...min, haftarah: { ...min.haftarah, book: "Isaiah" }, commentary: [] });
  assert.deepEqual(carryInto(otherBook, prior).added.filter(a => a.reading === "haftarah"), [], "a different haftarah (a special Shabbat) carries nothing");
});

test("See also: a parashah's short address; a festival's slug; added once to the calendar paragraph", () => {
  assert.equal(seeAlsoSentence({ slug: "2025-10-25-noach", cycle: "5786", parashahKey: "noach" }), "See also the 5786 sheet: haftarah.centralreform.org/5786/noach.");
  assert.equal(seeAlsoSentence({ slug: "2026-04-04-pesach-shabbat-chol-ha-moed", cycle: "5786", parashahKey: "pesach-shabbat-chol-ha-moed" }), "See also the 5786 sheet: haftarah.centralreform.org/2026-04-04-pesach-shabbat-chol-ha-moed.");
  const s = "See also the 5786 sheet: haftarah.centralreform.org/5786/noach.";
  assert.deepEqual(withSeeAlso({ calendar: "", setting: [] }, s), { calendar: s, setting: [] });
  assert.deepEqual(withSeeAlso({ calendar: "Read in place of X.", setting: [] }, s).calendar, `Read in place of X. ${s}`);
  assert.deepEqual(withSeeAlso({ calendar: ["One.", "Two."] }, s).calendar, ["One.", `Two. ${s}`]);
  assert.deepEqual(withSeeAlso(withSeeAlso({ calendar: "A." }, s), s).calendar, `A. ${s}`);
});

test("applyPrior: carry-forward is on by default (pointer, entries, sentence); --no-carry gives a fresh sheet the pointer only and leaves a kept sheet alone", async () => {
  const min = await load("week-min");
  const prior = priorFromSheet(min, { slug: "2025-10-25-noach", source: "web library" });
  const fresh = migrate3({ ...min, commentary: [] });
  const a = applyPrior(fresh, prior, { fresh: true });
  assert.deepEqual(a.sheet.prior, { slug: "2025-10-25-noach", hebrewEn: "29 Tishrei 5787", cycle: "5787" });
  assert.equal(a.added.length, 2);
  assert.ok(a.sheet.readings[0].commentary.every(e => e.status === "carried"));
  assert.match(a.sheet.openingNote.calendar, /See also the 5787 sheet: haftarah\.centralreform\.org\/5787\/bereshit\.$/);
  const kept = applyPrior(fresh, prior, {});
  assert.equal(kept.added.length, 2, "a kept sheet carries by default too");
  const again = applyPrior(a.sheet, prior, {});
  assert.deepEqual(again.added, []);
  assert.equal(again.changed, false, "a rerun changes nothing");
  const cut = structuredClone(a.sheet); cut.readings[0].commentary[0].status = "rejected";
  assert.deepEqual(applyPrior(cut, prior, {}).added, [], "an entry Daniel cut stays cut");
  const b = applyPrior(fresh, prior, { fresh: true, carry: false });
  assert.deepEqual(b.sheet.prior, a.sheet.prior);
  assert.equal(b.added.length, 0);
  assert.equal(b.sheet.openingNote.calendar, fresh.openingNote.calendar);
  const c = applyPrior(min, prior, { carry: false });
  assert.equal(c.sheet, min);
  assert.equal(c.changed, false);
});

test("no prior: the reason is kept, nothing is set on the sheet, and the digest says so", async () => {
  const none = noPrior(["haazinu"]);
  assert.deepEqual(none, { slug: null, parashahKey: "haazinu", searched: "web library" });
  const min = await load("week-min");
  const r = applyPrior(min, none, { fresh: true, carry: true });
  assert.equal(r.sheet, min);
  assert.match(lastTimeMarkdown(none, min), /^# Last time\n\nNo earlier sheet for haazinu in the web library\.\n$/);
  assert.match(lastTimeMarkdown(noPrior(["noach"], "the library index could not be read (HTTP 503)"), min), /Could not look: the library index could not be read \(HTTP 503\)\./);
  const failing = async () => new Response("down", { status: 503 });
  const looked = await lookupPrior({ keys: ["noach"], civil: "2026-10-17", fetchImpl: failing });
  assert.equal(looked.slug, null);
  assert.match(looked.error, /HTTP 503/);
});

test("lookupPrior reads the public library with GETs only, or a Drive folder when given", async () => {
  const notes = await load("week-notes");
  const calls = [];
  const fetchImpl = async (url, init = {}) => {
    calls.push({ url: String(url), method: init.method || "GET" });
    if (String(url).endsWith("/data/index.json")) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ weeks: [{ slug: "2025-10-04-bereshit-machar-chodesh", civil: "2025-10-04", parashahKeys: ["bereshit"] }] }), { status: 200 });
    if (String(url).endsWith("/data/weeks/2025-10-04-bereshit-machar-chodesh/sheet.json")) return new Response(JSON.stringify(notes), { status: 200 });
    return new Response("no", { status: 404 });
  };
  const p = await lookupPrior({ keys: ["bereshit"], civil: "2026-10-10", fetchImpl });
  assert.equal(p.slug, "2025-10-04-bereshit-machar-chodesh");
  assert.equal(p.source, "web library");
  assert.deepEqual(calls.map(c => c.method), ["GET", "GET"]);
  assert.ok(calls.every(c => c.url.startsWith("https://haftarah.centralreform.org/data/")));
  const dir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "prior-"));
  await writeFile(join(dir, "sheet.json"), JSON.stringify(notes));
  const q = await lookupPrior({ keys: ["bereshit"], civil: "2026-10-10", priorDir: dir, fetchImpl: async () => { throw new Error("no network for a folder"); } });
  assert.equal(q.source, "folder");
  assert.equal(q.slug, notes.slug);
  await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test("lastTimeMarkdown: what was printed, what of it is in this year's reading, the verses it never reached, what was carried", async () => {
  const min = await load("week-min");
  const prior = priorFromSheet(min, { slug: "2025-10-25-noach", source: "web library" });
  const now = applyPrior(migrate3({ ...min, commentary: [] }), prior, {}).sheet;
  const md = lastTimeMarkdown(prior, now);
  assert.match(md, /^# Last time: 29 Tishrei 5787 \(2025-10-25-noach, from the web library\)/);
  assert.match(md, /Haftarah I Samuel 20:18-20: 2 entries printed; 2 in this year's reading \(20:18 Rashi; 20:19 A fixture summary\)\./);
  assert.match(md, /Verses with no entry last time: 20:20\./);
  assert.match(md, /Carried into this sheet: 2 \(status "carried"; set "rejected" to cut\)\./);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `scripts/prior.mjs`**

```js
/**
 * prior.mjs — "Last time": the most recent earlier sheet for the same parashah, what it printed, and
 * what of it fits this year's readings. fetch.mjs writes prior.json every run and, unless --no-carry,
 * copies the fitting entries in (status "carried", Daniel cuts: carry-forward is on by default); digest.mjs prints the "Last time" section.
 *
 * The prior comes from the public library (index.json and that week's sheet.json, GET only: the
 * published copy, which has what printed and none of what was cut), or from a week folder restored
 * from Drive (--prior <dir>), which also has the rejected entries.
 */
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { join, basename, resolve } from "node:path";
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import { indexUrl, dataUrl } from "../site/app/contract.js";
import { weekKeys, isParashahKey } from "../site/app/parashot.js";
import { migrate3 } from "./migrate.mjs";

const L = createRequire(import.meta.url)("../template/lib.js");
export const SITE = "https://haftarah.centralreform.org";

const newestFirst = (a, b) => String(b.civil).localeCompare(String(a.civil)) || String(a.slug).length - String(b.slug).length || String(a.slug).localeCompare(String(b.slug));

/** The newest week before `civil` that shares a parashah key with `keys`; null when there is none. */
export function findPrior(index, { keys, civil }) {
  const want = new Set(keys || []);
  const rows = ((index && index.weeks) || []).filter(w => String(w.civil || "") < civil && weekKeys(w).some(k => want.has(k)));
  return rows.sort(newestFirst)[0] || null;
}

/** What prior.json keeps of an earlier sheet: per reading, the printed entries with ids and chapters, its glosses, and what was cut. */
export function priorFromSheet(sheet, { slug, source }) {
  const s = sheet.shabbat || {};
  return {
    slug, source,
    hebrewEn: s.hebrewEn || "",
    cycle: String((sheet.series && sheet.series.year) || (s.hebrewEn || "").split(/\s+/).pop() || ""),
    parashahKey: L.parashahKey((s.parashah && s.parashah.en) || ""),
    readings: L.readings(sheet).map(r => {
      // A schema-2 sheet's entries get the ids migrate --to 3 would give them; a schema-3 sheet's keep theirs.
      const list = L.assignEntryIds(structuredClone(r.commentary || []), r.id);
      const chapterOf = e => (e.chapter != null ? e.chapter : ((r.verses || []).find(v => v.verse === e.verse) || {}).chapter ?? null);
      const strip = ({ status, carried, ...e }) => ({ ...e, chapter: chapterOf(e) });
      return {
        id: r.id, kind: r.kind, ref: r.ref || "", book: r.book || "",
        entries: list.filter(e => e.status !== "rejected").map(strip),
        glosses: (r.glosses || []).filter(g => g.status !== "rejected").map(({ status, ...g }) => g),
        cut: list.filter(e => e.status === "rejected").map(e => ({ id: e.id, chapter: chapterOf(e), verse: e.verse, source: e.source || "", sourceRef: e.sourceRef || "" })),
      };
    }),
  };
}

/** prior.json when there is nothing to build on, and why. */
export function noPrior(keys, error = null) {
  const out = { slug: null, parashahKey: (keys && keys[0]) || "", searched: "web library" };
  if (error) out.error = error;
  return out;
}

/** The prior: a Drive-restored folder when given, else the public library. Never throws for a library it cannot reach. */
export async function lookupPrior({ keys, civil, priorDir = null, fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch, site = SITE }) {
  if (priorDir) {
    const sheet = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(priorDir, "sheet.json"), "utf8"));
    return priorFromSheet(sheet, { slug: sheet.slug || basename(resolve(priorDir)), source: "folder" });
  }
  const get = async url => {
    const res = await fetchImpl(url, { method: "GET", headers: { accept: "application/json" } });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
    return res.json();
  };
  let index;
  try { index = await get(`${site}${indexUrl()}`); }
  catch (err) { return noPrior(keys, `the library index could not be read (${err.message})`); }
  const row = findPrior(index, { keys, civil });
  if (!row) return noPrior(keys);
  try { return priorFromSheet(await get(`${site}${dataUrl(row.slug, "sheet.json")}`), { slug: row.slug, source: "web library" }); }
  catch (err) { return noPrior(keys, `${row.slug}'s sheet could not be read (${err.message})`); }
}

/** Copy each prior entry that falls in this year's reading of the same kind and book; ids kept; reruns add nothing. */
export function carryInto(sheet, prior) {
  if (!prior || !prior.slug) return { sheet, added: [] };
  const out = Number(sheet.schema) >= 3 ? structuredClone(sheet) : migrate3(sheet);
  const have = new Set(L.readings(out).flatMap(r => (r.commentary || []).map(e => e.id)).filter(Boolean));
  const added = [];
  for (const pr of prior.readings || []) {
    const r = (out.readings || []).find(x => x.kind === pr.kind && x.book === pr.book);
    if (!r) continue;
    const here = new Set((r.verses || []).map(v => `${v.chapter}:${v.verse}`));
    for (const e of pr.entries || []) {
      if (!e.id || have.has(e.id) || !here.has(`${e.chapter}:${e.verse}`)) continue;
      (r.commentary = r.commentary || []).push({ ...e, status: "carried", carried: prior.slug });
      have.add(e.id);
      added.push({ reading: r.id, id: e.id, chapter: e.chapter, verse: e.verse, source: e.source || "" });
    }
  }
  return { sheet: added.length ? out : sheet, added };
}

/** The opening note's proposed sentence (spec 5.4). A festival has no short address, so it links the slug. */
export function seeAlsoSentence(prior) {
  const path = isParashahKey(prior.parashahKey) ? `${prior.cycle}/${prior.parashahKey}` : prior.slug;
  return `See also the ${prior.cycle} sheet: ${SITE.replace(/^https:\/\//, "")}/${path}.`;
}

/** The opening note with the sentence at the end of its calendar paragraph, once. */
export function withSeeAlso(note, sentence) {
  const on = { ...(note || {}) };
  const cal = on.calendar;
  if ([].concat(cal || []).some(p => String(p).includes(sentence))) return on;
  if (Array.isArray(cal)) on.calendar = cal.length ? [...cal.slice(0, -1), `${cal[cal.length - 1]} ${sentence}`] : [sentence];
  else on.calendar = cal ? `${cal} ${sentence}` : sentence;
  return on;
}

/**
 * What fetch does with the prior. Carry-forward is on by default (Daniel cuts): the pointer, the fitting
 * entries and the "See also" sentence, on a new sheet and on a kept one (reruns add nothing). With
 * carry: false (--no-carry), a new sheet gets the pointer only and a kept sheet is left alone.
 */
export function applyPrior(sheet, prior, { carry = true, fresh = false } = {}) {
  if (!prior || !prior.slug || !(carry || fresh)) return { sheet, changed: false, added: [] };
  let out = sheet, added = [];
  const pointer = { slug: prior.slug, hebrewEn: prior.hebrewEn, cycle: prior.cycle };
  if (JSON.stringify(sheet.prior) !== JSON.stringify(pointer)) out = { ...out, prior: pointer };
  if (carry) {
    ({ sheet: out, added } = carryInto(out, prior));
    const note = withSeeAlso(out.openingNote, seeAlsoSentence(prior));
    if (JSON.stringify(note) !== JSON.stringify(out.openingNote)) out = { ...out, openingNote: note };
  }
  return { sheet: out, changed: out !== sheet, added };
}

// "20:2, 20:4–6": verse labels, consecutive runs in one chapter joined.
function runs(list) {
  const out = [];
  for (const [c, v] of list) {
    const last = out[out.length - 1];
    if (last && last.c === c && last.to === v - 1) last.to = v;
    else out.push({ c, from: v, to: v });
  }
  return out.map(x => (x.from === x.to ? `${x.c}:${x.from}` : `${x.c}:${x.from}–${x.to}`)).join(", ");
}

/** The digest's "Last time" section. */
export function lastTimeMarkdown(prior, sheet) {
  if (!prior || !prior.slug) {
    const why = prior && prior.error ? `Could not look: ${prior.error}.` : `No earlier sheet for ${(prior && prior.parashahKey) || "this parashah"} in the ${(prior && prior.searched) || "web library"}.`;
    return `# Last time\n\n${why}\n`;
  }
  const out = [`# Last time: ${prior.hebrewEn} (${prior.slug}, from the ${prior.source})`, ""];
  const now = sheet ? L.readings(sheet) : [];
  for (const pr of prior.readings || []) {
    const label = pr.kind === "torah" ? "Torah" : "Haftarah";
    const r = now.find(x => x.kind === pr.kind && x.book === pr.book);
    const here = new Set(((r && r.verses) || []).map(v => `${v.chapter}:${v.verse}`));
    const inRange = pr.entries.filter(e => here.has(`${e.chapter}:${e.verse}`));
    const outside = pr.entries.length - inRange.length;
    const named = inRange.map(e => `${e.chapter}:${e.verse} ${e.source}`).join("; ");
    out.push(`${label} ${pr.ref}: ${pr.entries.length} entr${pr.entries.length === 1 ? "y" : "ies"} printed; ${r ? `${inRange.length} in this year's reading${named ? ` (${named})` : ""}` : `this year's ${label.toLowerCase()} is a different passage`}${outside && r ? `; ${outside} outside it` : ""}.`);
    if (pr.cut && pr.cut.length) out.push(`Cut last time: ${pr.cut.map(e => `${e.chapter}:${e.verse} ${e.source}`).join("; ")}.`);
    if (r) {
      const covered = new Set(pr.entries.map(e => `${e.chapter}:${e.verse}`));
      const bare = (r.verses || []).filter(v => !covered.has(`${v.chapter}:${v.verse}`)).map(v => [v.chapter, v.verse]);
      if (bare.length) out.push(`Verses with no entry last time: ${runs(bare)}.`);
      const carried = (r.commentary || []).filter(e => e.carried === prior.slug).length;
      if (carried) out.push(`Carried into this sheet: ${carried} (status "carried"; set "rejected" to cut).`);
    }
    out.push("");
  }
  for (const r of now) if (!(prior.readings || []).some(pr => pr.kind === r.kind)) out.push(`${r.kind === "torah" ? "Torah" : "Haftarah"}: not on the ${prior.cycle} sheet.`, "");
  return out.join("\n").replace(/\n+$/, "\n");
}
```

### Task 9: `publish.mjs --reindex`: rebuild the index from the store, or backfill from a weeks folder (Sonnet)

**Files:**
- Modify: `scripts/publish.mjs`, `tests/publish.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `indexEntry`, `upsertIndex`, `cleanSheet`, `putBlob`, `storeIdFromToken`, `fetchIndex`, `loadWebConfig`, `STORE_ID`, `BLOB_API` (publish.mjs); `SLUG` (contract.js).
- Produces:
  - `STORE_ORIGIN = "https://wogdaqvpmtfjkrmy.public.blob.vercel-storage.com"` (STORE_ID's public origin)
  - `listBlobs({ token, prefix, fetchImpl, api }) → Promise<[{ pathname, url, uploadedAt, size }]>` (follows the cursor)
  - `weeksInListing(blobs) → Map<slug, { [file]: blob }>` (only week slugs)
  - `reindex({ from = null, dryRun = false, config = null, log, fetchImpl, api, store, now }) → Promise<{ index }>`: with no `from`, the store's listing is the record: one row per `weeks/<slug>/sheet.json`, rebuilt by `indexEntry`, keeping each row's earlier `published` time when it had one; rows without a sheet in the store are dropped and named. With `from` (a local weeks folder), each week found there is upserted into the current index and nothing else changes. The index is uploaded last; `dryRun` uploads nothing and needs no token for `from`.
  - CLI: `node scripts/publish.mjs --reindex [--from weeks] [--dry-run]`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** In `tests/publish.test.mjs`, add `listBlobs, weeksInListing, reindex, STORE_ORIGIN` to the import from `../scripts/publish.mjs`, and append:

```js
// A stub of the store for --reindex: a listing in two pages, public sheet.json files, the index.
function storeStub({ weeks, index = null }) {
  const calls = [];
  let stored = index;
  const blobs = Object.entries(weeks).flatMap(([slug, files]) => Object.keys(files).map(f => ({ pathname: `weeks/${slug}/${f}`, url: `${STORE}/weeks/${slug}/${f}`, uploadedAt: "2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z", size: 1 })));
  blobs.push({ pathname: "weeks/not-a-week/sheet.json", url: `${STORE}/weeks/not-a-week/sheet.json`, uploadedAt: "x", size: 1 });
  const fetchImpl = async (url, init = {}) => {
    const u = new URL(url);
    calls.push({ method: init.method || "GET", url: String(url), headers: init.headers || {} });
    if (u.hostname === "vercel.com" && (init.method || "GET") === "GET") {
      if (init.headers.authorization !== `Bearer ${TOKEN}`) return new Response("forbidden", { status: 403 });
      const first = !u.searchParams.get("cursor");
      const half = Math.ceil(blobs.length / 2);
      return new Response(JSON.stringify(first ? { blobs: blobs.slice(0, half), hasMore: true, cursor: "c2" } : { blobs: blobs.slice(half), hasMore: false }), { status: 200 });
    }
    if (init.method === "PUT") { stored = JSON.parse(init.body); return new Response(JSON.stringify({ url: `${STORE}/index.json`, pathname: "index.json" }), { status: 200 }); }
    if (u.pathname === "/index.json") return stored ? new Response(JSON.stringify(stored), { status: 200 }) : new Response("no", { status: 404 });
    const m = /^\/weeks\/([^/]+)\/sheet\.json$/.exec(u.pathname);
    if (m && weeks[m[1]]) return new Response(JSON.stringify(weeks[m[1]]["sheet.json"]), { status: 200 });
    return new Response("no", { status: 404 });
  };
  return { calls, fetchImpl, get stored() { return stored; } };
}

test("weeksInListing keeps week folders only, by slug and file", () => {
  const m = weeksInListing([{ pathname: "weeks/2026-10-17-noach/sheet.json" }, { pathname: "weeks/2026-10-17-noach/sheet-torah.pdf" }, { pathname: "index.json" }, { pathname: "weeks/junk/sheet.json" }]);
  assert.deepEqual([...m.keys()], ["2026-10-17-noach"]);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(m.get("2026-10-17-noach")), ["sheet.json", "sheet-torah.pdf"]);
  assert.equal(STORE_ORIGIN, STORE);
});

  const st = storeStub({ weeks: { "2026-10-10-bereshit-machar-chodesh": { "sheet.json": a, "sheet.pdf": 1 }, "2025-10-25-noach": { "sheet.json": b, "sheet.pdf": 1 } },
    index: { schema: 1, weeks: [{ slug: "2026-10-10-bereshit-machar-chodesh", civil: "2026-10-10", published: "2026-10-09T12:00:00Z" }, { slug: "2024-01-01-gone", civil: "2024-01-01" }] } });
  const lines = [];
  const { index } = await reindex({ config: { token: TOKEN }, fetchImpl: st.fetchImpl, store: STORE, log: l => lines.push(l), now: () => new Date("2026-10-15T20:00:00Z") });
  assert.deepEqual(index.weeks.map(w => w.slug), ["2026-10-10-bereshit-machar-chodesh", "2025-10-25-noach"]);
  assert.equal(index.weeks[0].published, "2026-10-09T12:00:00Z");
  assert.equal(index.weeks[1].published, "2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z");

  assert.equal(index.weeks[1].cycle, "5787");
  assert.deepEqual(st.stored, index, "uploaded");
  assert.ok(lines.some(l => /dropped +2024-01-01-gone/.test(l)));
  assert.equal(st.calls.filter(c => c.url.includes("cursor=c2")).length, 1, "the listing's second page was read");
  assert.ok(lines.every(l => !l.includes("s3cr3tpart")));
});

});
```

```js
/** The store's public origin (the host vercel.json's /data rewrite points at). */
export const STORE_ORIGIN = `https://${STORE_ID.toLowerCase()}.public.blob.vercel-storage.com`;
```
