After `fetchIndex` add:

```js
/** Every blob under `prefix`, following the listing's cursor. The token is never echoed. */
export async function listBlobs({ token, prefix, fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch, api = BLOB_API }) {
  const out = [];
  let cursor = null;
  do {
    const q = new URLSearchParams({ prefix, limit: "1000" });
    if (cursor) q.set("cursor", cursor);
    const headers = { authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "x-api-version": API_VERSION };
    const storeId = storeIdFromToken(token);
    if (storeId) headers["x-vercel-blob-store-id"] = storeId;
    let res;
    try { res = await fetchImpl(`${api}/?${q}`, { method: "GET", headers }); }
    catch (err) { throw new Error(`listing ${prefix}: network error: ${err.cause?.code || err.message}`); }
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`listing ${prefix}: Blob answered HTTP ${res.status}`);
    const page = await res.json();
    out.push(...(page.blobs || []));
    cursor = page.hasMore ? page.cursor : null;
  } while (cursor);
  return out;
}

/** Week folders in a listing: slug → { "sheet.json": blob, "sheet.pdf": blob, ... }; anything not a week slug is left out. */
export function weeksInListing(blobs) {
  const m = new Map();
  for (const b of blobs) {
    const x = /^weeks\/([^/]+)\/([^/]+)$/.exec(b.pathname || "");
    if (!x || !SLUG.test(x[1])) continue;
    if (!m.has(x[1])) m.set(x[1], {});
    m.get(x[1])[x[2]] = b;
  }
  return m;
}

/**
 * Rebuild index.json. With no `from`, the store's listing is the record: a row per weeks/<slug>/sheet.json,
 * made by indexEntry from the published sheet, keeping each week's earlier `published` time; rows whose
 * sheet is gone are dropped and named. With `from` (a local weeks folder), each week found there is
 * upserted into the current index and nothing else changes. The index is uploaded last.
 */
export async function reindex({ from = null, dryRun = false, config = null, log = console.log, fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch, api = BLOB_API, store = null, now = () => new Date() } = {}) {
  const stamp = now().toISOString().replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/, "Z");
  const origin = store || STORE_ORIGIN;
  const current = await fetchIndex(origin, fetchImpl);
  const before = new Map((current.weeks || []).map(w => [w.slug, w]));
  let index;
  if (from) {
    index = { schema: 2, updated: stamp, weeks: (current.weeks || []).slice() };
    for (const name of (await readdir(from)).sort()) {
      if (!SLUG.test(name) || !(await exists(join(from, name, "sheet.json")))) continue;
      const clean = cleanSheet(JSON.parse(await readFile(join(from, name, "sheet.json"), "utf8")));
      index = upsertIndex(index, indexEntry(clean, name, (before.get(name) || {}).published || stamp), stamp);
      log(`index    ${name}  (from ${join(from, name, "sheet.json")})`);
    }
  } else {
    if (!config || !config.token) throw new Error("--reindex from the store needs the Blob token (or use --from <weeks folder>)");
    const weeks = weeksInListing(await listBlobs({ token: config.token, prefix: "weeks/", fetchImpl, api }));
    index = { schema: 2, updated: stamp, weeks: [] };
    for (const [slug, files] of [...weeks].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))) {
      if (!files["sheet.json"]) { log(`skip     ${slug}  (no sheet.json in the store)`); continue; }
      const res = await fetchImpl(`${files["sheet.json"].url}?${Date.now()}`, { method: "GET", headers: { "cache-control": "no-cache" } });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${slug}/sheet.json: Blob answered HTTP ${res.status}; nothing was changed`);
      const published = (before.get(slug) || {}).published || files["sheet.json"].uploadedAt || stamp;
      index = upsertIndex(index, indexEntry(await res.json(), slug, published), stamp);
      log(`index    ${slug}`);
    }
    for (const slug of before.keys()) if (!weeks.has(slug)) log(`dropped  ${slug}  (in the old index, not in the store)`);
  }
  if (dryRun) { log(`index.json  (${index.weeks.length} weeks; dry run, nothing uploaded)`); return { index }; }
  const token = config && config.token;
  const id = storeIdFromToken(token);
  if (!id || id.toLowerCase() !== STORE_ID.toLowerCase()) throw new Error(`publish: the Blob token is for store ${id || "(unknown)"}, not the site's store ${STORE_ID}; nothing uploaded`);
  const body = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(index), "utf8");
  const r = await putBlob({ pathname: INDEX_PATH, body, contentType: "application/json", maxAge: MAX_AGE.json, token, fetchImpl, api });
  log(`web      ${INDEX_PATH}  ${human(body.length)}  ${r.url}  (${index.weeks.length} week${index.weeks.length === 1 ? "" : "s"})`);
  return { index };
}
```

### Task 10: The library's short addresses: `parashot.js` allow-list, `vercel.json`, `library.html` (Opus)

**Files:**
- Create: `site/app/library.js`, `site/library.html`, `site/app/boot-library.js`, `site/app/library-page.js`, `tests/library.test.mjs`
- Modify: `site/app/parashot.js`, `vercel.json`, `scripts/web-check.mjs`, `tests/contract.test.mjs`, `tests/web-build.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PARASHOT`, `COMBINED`, `TORAH_BOOKS`, `PROPHET_BOOKS`, `SPECIALS`, `keyOf`, `isParashahKey`, `parashahName`, `weekKeys` (Task 7); `indexUrl` (contract.js); `rowHtml` (landing.js); `applyStored`, `initSettings` (settings.js); `serveSite`, `attrs`, `expect` in `scripts/web-check.mjs`.
- Produces:
  - `site/app/parashot.js`: `aliasRewrites() → [{ source, destination: "/library.html" }]` (five: year + parashah, parashah, `/torah/<book>`, `/prophets/<book>`, `/special/<tag>`), `isAliasPath(pathname) → boolean` (the same allow-list).
  - `site/app/library.js`: `newestFirst(a, b)`; `parseAlias(pathname) → { kind: "week", year, key } | { kind: "torah"|"prophets", book } | { kind: "special", tag, en } | null`; `resolveWeekAlias(alias, weeks) → { slug } | { missing: { key, year, en } }`; `torahBookOf(row)`, `haftarahBookOf(row)`, `haftarahRefOf(row)`; `weeksFor(alias, weeks) → { title, weeks }`.
  - `site/library.html` + `library-page.js` `run()`: a week alias goes on to `/<slug>` with `location.replace` (the alias leaves no history entry; the canonical page loads with its own shell); with `?check=1` it stops and reports `data-check-resolved`. A week alias with no sheet says "No sheet for <Parashah> yet." / "No sheet for <Parashah> in <year>." (`data-check-missing`). A list alias shows its weeks with the landing's rows (`data-check-listed`).

Decision recorded here: a week alias is resolved in `library.html` and then loads the canonical page with `location.replace("/<slug>")` rather than `history.replaceState` in place, because the week page's shell is `sheet.html`; the reader's address bar ends on the permanent slug and the back button skips the alias, which is what the spec's replaceState was for.

- [ ] **Step 3: parashot.js, the allow-list.** Append to `site/app/parashot.js`:

```js
const alt = list => list.join("|");
const PARASHAH_ALIASES = () => [...PARASHOT.map(p => p.key), ...Object.keys(COMBINED)];

/**
 * vercel.json's rewrites for the library's short addresses, an explicit allow-list (any other path
 * stays the site's 404). tests/contract.test.mjs holds vercel.json to this. The week's own address,
 * /<date-slug>, stays the permanent page; these only lead to it.
 */
export function aliasRewrites() {
  const keys = alt(PARASHAH_ALIASES());
  return [
    { source: `/:year(\\d{4})/:parashah(${keys})`, destination: "/library.html" },
    { source: `/:parashah(${keys})`, destination: "/library.html" },
    { source: `/torah/:book(${alt(TORAH_BOOKS.map(keyOf))})`, destination: "/library.html" },
    { source: `/prophets/:book(${alt(PROPHET_BOOKS.map(keyOf))})`, destination: "/library.html" },
    { source: `/special/:tag(${alt(SPECIALS.map(s => s.tag))})`, destination: "/library.html" },
  ];
}

/** The same allow-list as a test of one path (web-check's local server routes with it). */
export function isAliasPath(pathname) {
  const re = new RegExp(`^/(?:(?:\\d{4}/)?(?:${alt(PARASHAH_ALIASES())})|torah/(?:${alt(TORAH_BOOKS.map(keyOf))})|prophets/(?:${alt(PROPHET_BOOKS.map(keyOf))})|special/(?:${alt(SPECIALS.map(s => s.tag))}))$`);
  return re.test(String(pathname == null ? "" : pathname));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `site/app/library.js`**

```js
// The library over index.json: the short addresses (/noach, /5787/noach, /torah/genesis,
// /prophets/isaiah, /special/shuva) and, from Task 11, the landing's views. Pure: no DOM. Every row
// is an index.json week; rows written before index v2 are read through weekKeys and fall back to
// their v1 fields (publish.mjs --reindex fills them in).
import { PARASHOT, COMBINED, TORAH_BOOKS, PROPHET_BOOKS, SPECIALS, keyOf, isParashahKey, parashahName, weekKeys } from "./parashot.js";

/** Newest Shabbat first; two sheets on one Shabbat: the plainer (shorter) slug first. */
export const newestFirst = (a, b) => String(b.civil || "").localeCompare(String(a.civil || "")) || String(a.slug).length - String(b.slug).length || String(a.slug).localeCompare(String(b.slug));

/** What a short address asks for, or null for any other path. */
export function parseAlias(pathname) {
  const p = String(pathname == null ? "" : pathname).replace(/\/+$/, "");
  let m = /^\/(\d{4})\/([a-z0-9-]+)$/.exec(p);
  if (m && isParashahKey(m[2])) return { kind: "week", year: m[1], key: m[2] };
  m = /^\/([a-z0-9-]+)$/.exec(p);
  if (m && isParashahKey(m[1])) return { kind: "week", year: null, key: m[1] };
  m = /^\/torah\/([a-z0-9-]+)$/.exec(p);
  if (m) { const book = TORAH_BOOKS.find(b => keyOf(b) === m[1]); if (book) return { kind: "torah", book }; }
  m = /^\/prophets\/([a-z0-9-]+)$/.exec(p);
  if (m) { const book = PROPHET_BOOKS.find(b => keyOf(b) === m[1]); if (book) return { kind: "prophets", book }; }
  m = /^\/special\/([a-z0-9-]+)$/.exec(p);
  if (m) { const s = SPECIALS.find(x => x.tag === m[1]); if (s) return { kind: "special", tag: s.tag, en: s.en }; }
  return null;
}

/** A week alias → the latest sheet for that parashah (that year's, with a year); a combined pair and its halves find each other. */
export function resolveWeekAlias(alias, weeks) {
  const want = COMBINED[alias.key] || [alias.key];
  const hits = (weeks || []).filter(w => weekKeys(w).some(k => want.includes(k)) && (!alias.year || String(w.cycle || "") === alias.year)).sort(newestFirst);
  return hits.length ? { slug: hits[0].slug } : { missing: { key: alias.key, year: alias.year, en: parashahName(alias.key) } };
}

/** The Torah book a week belongs to: its parashah's, else its Torah reading's; null for a festival without one. */
export function torahBookOf(w) {
  const keys = weekKeys(w);
  const p = PARASHOT.find(x => keys.includes(x.key));
  if (p) return p.book;
  const t = (w.readings || []).find(r => r.kind === "torah");
  return t ? t.book || null : null;
}
export const haftarahOf = w => (w.readings || []).find(r => r.kind === "haftarah") || null;
export const haftarahBookOf = w => (haftarahOf(w) || {}).book || w.book || null;
export const haftarahRefOf = w => (haftarahOf(w) || {}).ref || w.ref || "";

/** A list alias → its title and its weeks, newest first. */
export function weeksFor(alias, weeks) {
  const list = (weeks || []).slice().sort(newestFirst);
  if (alias.kind === "torah") return { title: `Torah: ${alias.book}`, weeks: list.filter(w => torahBookOf(w) === alias.book) };
  if (alias.kind === "prophets") return { title: `Haftarot from ${alias.book}`, weeks: list.filter(w => haftarahBookOf(w) === alias.book) };
  if (alias.kind === "special") return { title: alias.en, weeks: list.filter(w => (w.tags || []).includes(alias.tag)) };
  return { title: "", weeks: [] };
}
```

// The library shell's entry point: a file, so the Content-Security-Policy can forbid inline scripts.
import { run } from "./library-page.js";

Create `site/app/library-page.js`:

```js
// The library's short addresses. vercel.json sends exactly the allow-listed ones (parashot.js) here;
// this page resolves them against index.json. A week alias goes on to its permanent page, /<date-slug>,
// with location.replace, so the alias leaves no history entry. A list alias (a book, a special
// Shabbat) shows its weeks here with the landing's rows. ?check=1 reports instead of moving on.
import { applyStored, initSettings } from "./settings.js";
import { indexUrl } from "./contract.js";
import { parseAlias, resolveWeekAlias, weeksFor } from "./library.js";
import { rowHtml } from "./landing.js";

export async function run() {
  const esc = window.HaftarahLib.escapeHtml;
  const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
  const html = document.documentElement;
  const check = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("check") === "1";
  const errors = [];
  if (check) {
    addEventListener("error", e => errors.push(e.message));
    document.addEventListener("securitypolicyviolation", e => errors.push(`CSP blocked ${e.blockedURI || "inline"} (${e.violatedDirective})`));
  }
  const done = attrs => {
    if (!check) return;
    Object.assign(html.dataset, attrs);
    html.dataset.checkErrors = String(errors.length) + (errors.length ? ": " + errors[0] : "");
    html.dataset.checkDone = "1";
  };
  const say = msg => { $("status").innerHTML = msg; $("status").hidden = false; };
  applyStored();
  initSettings({ host: $("settings"), button: $("aaBtn"), trope: false, langs: false });

  const alias = parseAlias(location.pathname.toLowerCase());
  if (!alias) { say(`No sheet at this address. <a href="/">All sheets</a>`); return done({ checkMissing: "address" }); }
  let weeks;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(indexUrl());
    if (res.status === 404) weeks = [];
    else if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
    else { const idx = await res.json(); weeks = idx && Array.isArray(idx.weeks) ? idx.weeks : []; }
  } catch {
    say(`The sheets could not be loaded; try again in a minute. <a href="/">All sheets</a>`);
    return done({ checkMissing: "index" });
  }

  if (alias.kind === "week") {
    const r = resolveWeekAlias(alias, weeks);
    if (r.slug) {
      if (check) return done({ checkResolved: r.slug });
      location.replace(`/${r.slug}${location.hash}`);
      return;
    }
    const m = r.missing;
    document.title = `${m.en} · Haftarah`;
    say(`${m.year ? `No sheet for ${esc(m.en)} in ${esc(m.year)}.` : `No sheet for ${esc(m.en)} yet.`} <a href="/">All sheets</a>`);
    return done({ checkMissing: m.key });
  }

  const list = weeksFor(alias, weeks);
  document.title = `${list.title} · Haftarah`;
  $("libTitle").textContent = list.title;
  $("weeks").innerHTML = list.weeks.map(w => rowHtml(w, false, esc)).join("");
  $("index").hidden = false;
  if (!list.weeks.length) say(`No sheets here yet. <a href="/">All sheets</a>`);
  done({ checkListed: String(list.weeks.length), checkListTitle: list.title });
}
```

  const all = gridHtml(g, esc) + bookHtml(byBook(WEEKS), esc) + specialHtml(specials(WEEKS), esc);

  assert.match(bookHtml(byBook(WEEKS), esc), /<a href="\/torah\/genesis">Genesis<\/a><span class="n">3 sheets<\/span>/);
  assert.match(specialHtml(specials(WEEKS), esc), /<a href="\/special\/chanukah">Shabbat Chanukah<\/a><span class="n">1 sheet<\/span>/);
});

- [ ] **Step 3: library.js, the views.** Append to `site/app/library.js`:

```js
/** The by-parashah grid: five book bands, a cell per parashah in Torah order with its years and latest sheet. */
export function parashahGrid(weeks, { currentSlug = null } = {}) {
  return TORAH_BOOKS.map(book => ({
    book,
    cells: PARASHOT.filter(p => p.book === book).map(p => {
      const mine = (weeks || []).filter(w => weekKeys(w).includes(p.key)).sort(newestFirst);
      return {
        key: p.key, en: p.en, he: p.he,
        years: [...new Set(mine.map(w => String(w.cycle || "")).filter(Boolean))].sort(),
        latest: mine.length ? mine[0].slug : null,
        current: !!currentSlug && mine.some(w => w.slug === currentSlug),
      };
    }),
  }));
}

/** The chapters a list of refs covers, sorted: "Isaiah 54:1–55:5" → 54, 55; "…; 29:22–23" adds 29. */
export function chaptersOf(refs) {
  const out = new Set();
  for (const ref of refs || []) for (const m of String(ref).matchAll(/(\d+):\d+(?:[–-](\d+):\d+)?/g)) {
    const a = Number(m[1]), z = m[2] ? Number(m[2]) : a;
    for (let c = a; c <= z; c++) out.add(c);
  }
  return [...out].sort((a, b) => a - b);
}

/** The by-book door: the Torah books with their weeks; the haftarah books that hold sheets, Joshua to Malachi, with the chapters covered. */
export function byBook(weeks) {
  const list = (weeks || []).slice().sort(newestFirst);
  return {
    torah: TORAH_BOOKS.map(book => ({ book, key: keyOf(book), weeks: list.filter(w => torahBookOf(w) === book) })),
    prophets: PROPHET_BOOKS.map(book => {
      const ws = list.filter(w => haftarahBookOf(w) === book);
      return { book, key: keyOf(book), weeks: ws, chapters: chaptersOf(ws.map(haftarahRefOf)) };
    }).filter(b => b.weeks.length),
  };
}

/** The special-Shabbat door: the specials that hold sheets, in SPECIALS order. */
export function specials(weeks) {
  const list = (weeks || []).slice().sort(newestFirst);
  return SPECIALS.map(s => ({ tag: s.tag, en: s.en, weeks: list.filter(w => (w.tags || []).includes(s.tag)) })).filter(s => s.weeks.length);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `site/app/library-view.js`**

```js
// The landing's doors as markup: pure functions of library.js's views, escaped with the caller's esc.
// No style attributes (the CSP forbids them); every link is a real address the library resolves.
const sheets = n => `${n} sheet${n === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;

export function cellHtml(c, esc) {
  const names = `<span class="en">${esc(c.en)}</span><span class="he" lang="he" dir="rtl">${esc(c.he)}</span>`;
  if (!c.latest) return `<li class="cell empty">${names}</li>`;
  const said = `${sheets(c.years.length)}: ${c.years.join(", ")}${c.current ? "; this week" : ""}`;
  return `<li class="cell${c.current ? " current" : ""}"><a href="/${esc(c.key)}">${names}<span class="dots" aria-hidden="true">${c.years.map(y => `<span class="dot" title="${esc(y)}"></span>`).join("")}</span><span class="sr">${esc(said)}</span></a></li>`;
}

export function gridHtml(grid, esc) {
  return grid.map(b => `<section class="band"><h3>${esc(b.book)}</h3><ol class="cells">${b.cells.map(c => cellHtml(c, esc)).join("")}</ol></section>`).join("");
}

export function bookHtml(books, esc) {
  const torah = books.torah.map(b => `<li><a href="/torah/${esc(b.key)}">${esc(b.book)}</a><span class="n">${sheets(b.weeks.length)}</span></li>`).join("");
  const prophets = books.prophets.map(b => `<li><a href="/prophets/${esc(b.key)}">${esc(b.book)}</a><span class="ch">${b.chapters.join(", ")}</span><span class="n">${sheets(b.weeks.length)}</span></li>`).join("");
  return `<div class="books"><section><h3>Torah</h3><ol>${torah}</ol></section><section><h3>Prophets</h3><ol>${prophets}</ol></section></div>`;
}

export function specialHtml(list, esc) {
  return `<ol class="specials">${list.map(s => `<li><a href="/special/${esc(s.tag)}">${esc(s.en)}</a><span class="n">${sheets(s.weeks.length)}</span></li>`).join("")}</ol>`;
}
```

Replace `rowHtml` with:

```js
export function rowHtml(w, current, esc) {
  // A week with a Torah reading: two lines, the Torah's ref then the haftarah's.
  const torah = (w.readings || []).find(r => r.kind === "torah");
  const refs = torah
    ? `<span class="r two"><span>${esc(torah.ref || "")}</span><span>${esc(w.ref || "")}</span></span>`
    : `<span class="r">${esc(w.ref || "")}</span>`;
  return `<li${current ? ' class="current"' : ""}><span class="d">${esc(w.hebrewEn || "")}</span><a href="/${esc(w.slug)}">${esc(w.shabbat || w.slug)}</a>${refs}<span class="c">${esc(w.civilDisplay || w.civil || "")}</span></li>`;
}

/** The four doors over one list: aria-pressed buttons, one panel shown. */
export function initDoors(host, { initial = "week", remember = () => {} } = {}) {
  const buttons = [...host.querySelectorAll("[data-door]")];
  const show = door => {
    for (const b of buttons) {
      const on = b.dataset.door === door;
      b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(on));
      host.querySelector(`#door-${b.dataset.door}`).hidden = !on;
    }
  };
  for (const b of buttons) b.addEventListener("click", () => { show(b.dataset.door); remember(b.dataset.door); });
  show(buttons.some(b => b.dataset.door === initial) ? initial : "week");
  return { show };
}
```

});

- [ ] **Step 3: lib.js.** In `template/lib.js`, after `assignEntryIds` (before the `// The incipit fits a 3.5in box…` comment), add:

```js
  // One reading's model for the web page: sheetModel on readingView(data, readingId). sheetModel gives
  // every reading's last verse the haftarah's closing cadence; a Torah reading ends on a plain sof
  // pasuk, so its last verse and its trope legend are set right here. A haftarah's model is sheetModel's.
  function readingModel(data, readingId, opts) {
    const r = readings(data).find(x => x.id === readingId) || primaryReading(data);
    const m = sheetModel(readingView(data, r ? r.id : undefined), opts);
    if (r && r.kind === "torah") {
      const last = m.verses[m.verses.length - 1];
      if (last) { last.heHtml = last.heHtml.replace(/\btr-sof-haftarah\b/g, "tr-sof-pasuk"); last.final = false; }
      const keys = new Set(m.front.families.map(x => x.key).filter(k => k !== "sof-haftarah"));
      if (m.verses.length) keys.add("sof-pasuk");
      m.front.families = TROPE_FAMILIES.filter(([k]) => keys.has(k)).map(([key, name]) => ({ key, name }));
    }
    m.reading = r ? { id: r.id, kind: r.kind, selection: r.selection || null } : null;
    return m;
  }
```

  // Every reading with verses, in the sheet's order (a Torah selection first, then the haftarah). The
  // week is known by its primary reading, the haftarah: the title, the hero, the end matter. Each
  // reading's model comes from readingModel (sheetModel on that reading; a Torah reading ends on sof

  const reading = L.primaryReading(data);
  if (!reading) return fail(`This sheet could not be loaded. The printed sheets are on <a href="${DRIVE}">Google Drive</a>.`);
  const parts = L.readings(data).filter(r => (r.verses || []).length).map(r => ({ reading: r, model: L.readingModel(data, r.id, { voices: voicesDict }) }));
  const model = (parts.find(p => p.reading.id === reading.id) || { model: L.readingModel(data, reading.id, { voices: voicesDict }) }).model;
  const multi = parts.length > 1;
  const torahPart = parts.find(p => p.reading.kind === "torah") || null;
  const highlights = Array.isArray(data.highlights) ? data.highlights : [];
  document.title = model.title;
  if (torahPart) settings.addDownload(dataUrl(slug, PDF_FILES.torah), "Download the Torah sheet (PDF)");

  // ---- front page: the sheet introduces itself (with two readings, the legend and the voices cover both) ----
  const f = model.front, prov = f.provenance;
  const used = L.REGISTER_ORDER.filter(k => parts.some(p => p.model.front.legend.used.includes(k)));
  const hasKeys = parts.some(p => p.model.front.legend.hasKeys);
  const hasKetiv = parts.some(p => p.model.front.legend.hasKetiv);
  const famKeys = new Set(parts.flatMap(p => p.model.front.families.map(x => x.key)));
  const families = L.TROPE_FAMILIES.filter(([k]) => famKeys.has(k)).map(([key, name]) => ({ key, name }));
  const voices = multi ? L.voicesFor(parts.flatMap(p => p.reading.commentary || []), voicesDict, x => !x || x.status !== "rejected") : f.voices;
  const refs = multi
    ? parts.map(p => `<span class="ref">${p.reading.kind === "torah" ? "Torah" : "Haftarah"} · ${esc(p.model.ref)}</span>`).join("<br>")
    : `<span class="ref">${esc(model.ref)}</span>`;
  const pdfs = torahPart
    ? `The printed sheets (PDF): <a class="pdf" href="${dataUrl(slug, PDF_FILES.torah)}">Torah</a> · <a class="pdf" href="${dataUrl(slug, PDF_FILES.haftarah)}">Haftarah</a>`
    : `<a class="pdf" href="${dataUrl(slug, "sheet.pdf")}">The printed sheet (PDF)</a>`;
  const prior = data.prior && data.prior.slug ? `<br><a class="prior" href="/${esc(data.prior.slug)}">Last time: the ${esc(data.prior.cycle || "")} sheet</a>` : "";
  const front = $("front");
  front.innerHTML = `
    <div class="hero">
      <img class="mural" src="/mural.png" alt="The Siona Benjamin floor mural at Central Reform Congregation">
      <div class="incipit-block">
        <h1 class="incipit" lang="he" dir="rtl">${f.incipit.heHtml}</h1>
        <p class="incipit-en">${esc(f.incipit.en)}</p>
        <p class="reading">${refs}<br>${esc(model.shabbatName)}<br>
          <span class="dates"><span class="he-date" lang="he" dir="rtl">${esc(model.shabbat.hebrew)}</span> · ${esc(model.shabbat.civilDisplay)}</span><br>
          ${pdfs}${prior}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="opening">
      ${f.openingNote.calendar.map(p => `<p>${p}</p>`).join("")}
      ${f.openingNote.setting.length ? `<div class="setting">${f.openingNote.setting.map(p => `<p>${p}</p>`).join("")}</div>` : ""}
    </div>
    <div class="legend">${L.REGISTER_ORDER.map(k => `<span class="reg reg-${k}${used.includes(k) ? "" : " unused"}">${SIG[k]}${NAMES[k]}</span>`).join("")}
      <span${hasKeys ? "" : ' class="unused"'}><span class="k">a–z</span>translators’ notes (JPS) and word notes: tap a letter</span>
      ${hasKetiv ? `<span><span class="k">[ ] ( )</span>read / written: where tradition reads a word differently from its spelling</span>` : ""}
      <span class="trope-legend"><span class="label">Chanter’s copy · trope phrases by family</span>${families.map(x => `<span class="tr tr-${x.key}">${esc(x.name)}</span>`).join(" ")}</span>
    </div>`;
  if (voices.length) front.append(el("div", "voices", `<h2>Voices on this sheet</h2><p>${voices.map(v => `<span class="voice"><span class="name">${esc(v.name)}</span>${v.refs && v.refs.length ? ` (${esc(v.refs.join(", "))})` : ""}${v.line ? ` ${esc(v.line)}` : ""}</span>`).join('<span class="sep"> · </span>')}</p>`));

- [ ] **Step 5: DESIGN.md.** At the end of `## The web sheet (screen)`, add:

```markdown
### The library
The landing's index has four doors, pill buttons 44px high in a row (ink-2 outline; the open one filled ink on paper): By week, By parashah, By book, Special Shabbatot. By week is the list as before; a week with a Torah reading sets its two refs on two lines, Torah first. By parashah is five bands, one per book of the Torah, each a grid of cells at least 8.5rem wide: the English name, the Hebrew under it in Ezra SIL ink-2, and a 6px rubric dot per year held. An empty cell is the same box in the unused grey, not a link. This week's cell has a 1.5px rubric ring inside its edge and says "this week" to a screen reader; the dots' years are read out as words. By book is two columns, the Torah books and the haftarah books with the chapters covered in ink-2 lining figures; Special Shabbatot is a list. Each row's count reads "3 sheets". The short addresses (/noach, /torah/genesis, …) use the same rows under a plain heading.

### Two readings
A week with a Torah selection shows it first. Each reading opens with a heading in rubric at 1.05rem, 600: "Torah" or "Haftarah", then its ref in ink at 500 and its selection in ink-2 italic; a .5px rule above every heading but the first. Verse rows, keys (one letter series per reading), the rail and the presenter run on through both. The hero lists both refs on two lines ("Torah · Genesis 6:9–7:16", "Haftarah · Isaiah 54:1–55:5") and both printed sheets; "Last time: the 5786 sheet" sits under them in ink-2, rubric on hover, when there is one.

### The Torah sheet in print
`sheet-torah.pdf` is the same template as the haftarah's, page for page, with three differences set by the build option `reading: "torah"`: the front page's head reads "Torah · <date>", the running heads give the Torah book and range, and the last verse ends on a plain sof pasuk, not the haftarah's closing cadence.
```

The six carried-over items → Task 1. Not in Wave 2, and not planned: words, translations, links, map, timeline, audio, PWA, MCP, a combined PDF, a full-parashah web view, search, the spec 4.6 `hasAudio`/`hasWords`/`hasLinks` flags and the stored `library` block (the site derives every view from `weeks[]`).
- **Placeholders:** none. The golden values are recorded by commands (Task 1 Step 7, Task 6 Steps 8-9) whose expected diffs are stated; `tests/fixtures/week-torah/sheet.json` is generated by a committed helper and held to it by a test; the `vercel.json` entries are written out and held to `aliasRewrites()` by a test.
- **Type and name consistency:** `readingView(data, id)`, `readings`, `primaryReading` as Wave 1 left them; `lintSheet(file, { voices, fix, customs, only })` in Tasks 2, 6; `loadCustoms(id)` in Tasks 2, 4, 6; `leyningOf`/`parsePick`/`defaultPick`/`selectTorah`/`aliyotLines`/`aliyotMarkdown` in Tasks 3, 4, 5; `READING_FILES`/`HTML_FILES`/`PDF_FILES`/`TROPE_HTML`/`PRIOR_FILE` in Tasks 4-8, 12; `mergeWeek(existing, { torah, leyning }) → { sheet, changed, notes }` and the `sheet`/`write` variables that Task 8's insertion uses; `weekDigest({ sheet, readings, prior })` in Tasks 5, 8; `parashahKeysOf`, `weekKeys`, `weekTags`, `isParashahKey`, `parashahName`, `keyOf` in Tasks 7, 8, 10, 11; `newestFirst`, `torahBookOf`, `haftarahBookOf`, `haftarahRefOf` in Tasks 10, 11; `makeWeekTorah(weekMin)` in Tasks 2, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 12; rows `{ v, row, app, keys, reading, model }` in Task 12 and panel.js's `page.rows`; web-check's `?check=1` attributes match the pages' dataset keys (`resolved`, `missing`, `listed`, `doors`, `cells`, `held`, `door-switch`, `heads`, `pdfs`, `prior`).
- **Shared-file order:** `fetch.mjs` (Tasks 1, 4, 8: Task 4 replaces `main` whole; Task 8 inserts before its `// 5. Write sheet.json.` line), `digest.mjs` (1, 5, 8), `publish.mjs` (7, 8, 9), `web-check.mjs` (10, 11, 12: insertions before `} finally { server.close(); }`, after the landing's hero line, and line-for-line replacements), `README.md` (1, 6, 8, 13). Each task quotes text as the tasks before it leave it.
- **Review Focus:** all five pinned (Tasks 7, 8, 10 for the double parashah; Task 3 and Task 7 for the special Shabbat; Task 8 and Task 12's web-check line for no prior; Task 7 and the golden for a schema-2 week; Task 10 and its web-check lines for an alias with no sheet).
